### [1] [Reducing Representational Interference in Retrieval Embeddings with Trainable Prefix Tokens](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07298)
*Seongtae Hong,Youngjoon Jang,Jungseob Lee,Chanjun Park,Heuiseok Lim*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 通过在嵌入模型输入前添加可训练 [DUMP]  token，在不改变架构、池化与损失的前提下缓解token级残差信息对检索表示的干扰，稳定提升检索性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 嵌入模型将变长文本压缩为定长向量时，语义内容会与残差式token级信息（如attention sink产生的伪信息）纠缠，污染最终检索表示。

Method: 在训练阶段于输入前拼接可训练[DUMP] token；保持模型结构、池化策略与对比检索损失不变，仅用标准对比检索目标优化这些前缀token，使其在编码时与常规token交互，吸收/稀释低信息量token信号。

Result: 在标准检索与长上下文基准上，编码器与解码器两类嵌入模型均一致优于微调基线；分析显示[DUMP] token改变了与低信息token相关的token级激活与注意力模式。

Conclusion: 可训练前缀token是一种轻量、即插即用的手段，可改善检索表示质量。

Abstract: Embedding models compress variable-length text into fixed-size vector representations, but this compression can entangle semantic content with residual token-level information, such as artifacts arising from attention sink behavior. We propose a simple method that prepends trainable [DUMP] tokens to the input during embedding model training. The method keeps the model architecture, pooling strategy, and training loss unchanged, while optimizing the added tokens through the standard contrastive retrieval objective. By introducing trainable prefix positions that interact with regular tokens during encoding, [DUMP] tokens help reduce the influence of residual or uninformative token-level signals on the final retrieval representation. Experiments on standard retrieval and long-context benchmarks show that our method consistently improves over finetuned baselines across encoder and decoder embedding models. Further analysis shows that [DUMP] tokens alter token-level activation and attention patterns associated with low-information tokens. These results suggest that trainable prefix tokens provide a lightweight way to improve retrieval representations.

</details>


### [2] [CacheGraft: Enhancing Retrieval-Augmented Generation via Latent-Space Document Enrichment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07393)
*Vihan Lakshman,Peter Baile Chen,Zhuohan Gu,Michael Cafarella*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文提出CacheGraft：在离线阶段将语料段落编码为大模型（4B）与小模型（0.6B）融合后的KV缓存，查询时无需大模型即可注入小模型，实现隐空间增强的RAG。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: KV缓存间的缓存到缓存翻译此前主要用于实时智能体通信；作者认为其价值可外延到检索增强生成（RAG），尤其是推理端只能部署小模型的计算受限场景。

Method: 基于Cache-to-Cache（C2C）融合框架，离线为每个语料段落构建sharer（4B）与receiver（0.6B）之间的融合KV缓存表示；查询时直接将该缓存注入0.6B接收模型，无需再运行大模型。fuser未在检索数据上训练（零样本）。

Result: 在五个QA基准、两个独立本地评测judge下，CacheGraft在30组对比中的29组优于纯文本receiver基线，其中17组统计显著；且增益为零样本（fuser未针对检索数据训练）。

Conclusion: 隐空间文档增强是提升RAG性能的有前景方向，特别适合推理端仅能部署小模型的计算受限环境；但结果仍是初步的，需更广泛验证。

Abstract: Recent work in machine learning has demonstrated that transformer-based language model agents can communicate state not only through text, but also directly in latent space via key-value (KV) caches. In this paper, we argue that such cache-to-cache translation can provide utility beyond the original motivating application of real-time agent communication and can instead be utilized for retrieval-augmented generation (RAG). Building on the Cache-to-Cache (C2C) fusion framework, our proposed system CacheGraft encodes each corpus passage offline into a fused KV cache representation between a larger sharer model (4B parameters in our experiments) and a smaller receiver model (0.6B parameters) such that this cache can be injected into the receiver at query time with no need for the larger model. Across five QA benchmarks and two independent locally-run judges, CacheGraft improves answer quality over the text-only receiver baseline in 29 out of 30 comparisons, of which 17 of the 30 are statistically significant. Notably, these gains are zero-shot with respect to the fuser, which was never trained on retrieval data. These initial results suggest that latent-space document enrichment may be a promising avenue for enhancing the performance of RAG systems, particularly in compute-constrained settings where it is only feasible to deploy a small model at inference time.

</details>


### [3] [BRIDGE: Bridging the Semantic Quantization Gap for Generative Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07425)
*Yatong Xiao,Mingjie Sun,Peng Wang,Wenjin Wu,Peng Jiang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 论文识别生成式推荐中RQ-VAE语义ID生成范式存在"语义量化鸿沟"(SQG)，提出BRIDGE框架从三个维度弥合该鸿沟，在三个Amazon基准与线上A/B实验中均取得稳定提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式推荐通过自回归生成语义ID(SID)检索物品，但RQ-VAE从富连续信号(码本质心、局部邻域几何、层级熵)导出SID后，解码器仅接收离散标识符，丢失了塑造SID的语义结构，形成被忽视的语义量化鸿沟(SQG)：①grounding gap(目标侧信息有限时缺乏语义锚点)；②neighborhood gap(token级监督丢弃物品间相似性并加剧曝光偏差)；③difficulty gap(各层计算均匀分配，而生成难度随深度递减)。

Method: 提出BRIDGE，包含三个组件：(1)码本接地隐推理——在SID生成前将冻结量化器质心作为语义锚点注入；(2)特权EMA自蒸馏——由教师向同策略(on-policy)学生迁移邻域感知知识，保留物品间几何并缓解曝光偏差；(3)努力感知循环——为难度较高的早期层级分配更多计算，随深度递增逐步减少。

Result: 在三个Amazon基准上，相较最强基线，Recall@10与NDCG@10平均提升10.9%与9.7%(最高12.6%与10.7%)；在某大规模电商广告平台5%流量的为期一周在线A/B实验中，eCPM提升0.65%。

Conclusion: 语义量化鸿沟是生成式推荐的关键瓶颈；BRIDGE通过码本接地隐推理、特权EMA自蒸馏与努力感知循环分别对应三类鸿沟，离线与线上实验验证了其有效性。

Abstract: Generative recommendation retrieves items by autoregressively generating Semantic IDs(SIDs), where an RQ-VAE quantizes each item into a hierarchical SID sequence that the decoder generates level by level. We identify an overlooked bottleneck in this paradigm: the Semantic Quantization Gap(SQG). Quantization derives SIDs from rich continuous signals, including codebook centroids, local neighborhood geometry, and level-wise entropy. However, the decoder receives only discrete identifiers and loses the semantic structure that shaped them. This mismatch manifests in three ways: a grounding gap, where the decoder lacks semantic anchors when target-side information is limited; a neighborhood gap, where token-level supervision discards inter-item similarity and exacerbates exposure bias; and a difficulty gap, where computation is allocated uniformly despite generation difficulty diminishing with depth.
  To bridge the SQG, we propose BRIDGE. Codebook-grounded latent reasoning injects frozen quantizer centroids as semantic anchors before SID generation. Privileged EMA self-distillation transfers neighborhood-aware knowledge from a teacher to an on-policy student, preserving inter-item geometry while mitigating exposure bias. Effort-aware recurrence allocates more computation to the challenging early levels and progressively reduces it with depth. Across three Amazon benchmarks, BRIDGE improves Recall@10 and NDCG@10 by 10.9% and 9.7% on average over the strongest baseline (up to 12.6% and 10.7%). In a one-week online A/B experiment on a large-scale e-commerce advertising platform with 5% traffic, BRIDGE improves eCPM by 0.65%.

</details>


### [4] [OptST: Optimizing Semantic Tokenization for LLM-Enhanced Recommendation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07523)
*Hanchong Chen,Jie Liu,Fuyuan Lyu,Dugang Liu,Lingjie Li,Xiuqiang He,Xing Tang*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出OptST，让预训练语义分词器在推荐任务监督下自适应：用受锚正则约束的低秩形变修正token分配，并用交互条件前缀控制残差细节的暴露深度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 两阶段管线中分词器先训、推荐器后训，token分配被当作固定特征，下游只能学会使用既定索引而无法修正其语义划分；同时最近码分配是离散的、无约束自适应会破坏预训练几何，且固定token深度对所有交互给出同一语义分辨率。

Method: OptST在不动预训练编码器与基础码本的前提下，学习一个由锚正则限制漂移的低秩形变来修正token分配；推荐监督通过直通估计器传递到该变换，预测时仍保留硬最近码分配；并选取交互条件前缀，控制暴露给推荐器的残差细节量。

Result: 在四个公开数据集上，OptST在传统与LLM增强基线中取得最高AUC，且改进在不同推荐骨干上持续存在。

Conclusion: 任务感知的语义分词可作为LLM派生画像与下游推荐模型之间的有效接口。

Abstract: Semantic information can enrich the identifiers and structured fields used by recommender systems. Semantic tokenization provides a structured representation of this information by converting LLM-derived user profiles into discrete tokens. In the common two-stage pipeline, the tokenizer is trained before the recommender. Its token assignments are then treated as fixed input features during recommendation training. As a result, the downstream recommender can learn how to use the assigned indices, but it cannot revise the semantic partition that produced them. Making this partition task-aware is difficult because nearest-code assignment is discrete, and unconstrained adaptation may also distort the geometry learned during pretraining. Moreover, a fixed token depth exposes the same semantic resolution to every interaction. We thus propose OptST (Optimizing Semantic Tokenization), which makes a pretrained tokenizer responsive to downstream recommendation. To revise token assignments without directly updating the pretrained encoder or base codebooks, OptST learns a low-rank deformation whose drift is limited by an anchor regularizer. Recommendation supervision reaches this transformation through a straight-through estimator, while prediction retains hard nearest-code assignments. Beyond adapting the assignments themselves, OptST selects an interaction-conditioned prefix to control how much residual detail is exposed to the recommender. Experiments on four public datasets show that OptST achieves the highest AUC among conventional and LLM-enhanced baselines. Its improvements also persist across different recommendation backbones. These results establish task-aware semantic tokenization as an effective interface between LLM-derived profiles and downstream recommendation models.

</details>


### [5] [What Gets Hacked in a Collaborative Reward for LLM Recommenders: The Part That Does Not Depend on the User](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07588)
*Zhuangsheng Li,Liang Dong,Yu Wu,Dailin Li,Yu Xia,Yunhan C. Zhang,Rui Cai,Yi Wu,Julian McAuley*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该论文研究用协同过滤(CF)分数作为强化学习奖励训练LLM推荐器时出现的"曝光塌缩"问题，将其归因于CF分数中的物品均值分量，并提出逐物品中心化与逐物品标准化两种奖励校正方法，在保持排序精度的情况下显著提升目录覆盖率和曝光均衡性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CF分数能为LLM推荐器提供细粒度、个性化的强化学习奖励，但会导致曝光塌缩：尽管CF模型本身在不同用户间给出多样的top推荐，用其奖励优化的LLM却集中于更少的物品上。论文旨在诊断该塌缩的成因并加以修正。

Method: 将每个CF分数分解为物品均值分量（该物品在用户历史中的平均CF分数）与残差分量（当前用户相对均值的偏差），通过受控干预识别物品均值分量是曝光塌缩的成因。据此提出两种奖励校正：(1) 逐物品中心化，减去每个物品的估计均值分数；(2) 逐物品标准化，进一步用估计标准差对残差重新缩放。

Result: 在两个数据集、两种标识符方案上的实验表明，两种校正均大幅提升目录覆盖率与曝光均衡度；相比未校正的CF奖励训练，跨用户排名第一的不同物品数量最高提升9.8倍，同时排序精度保持可比。

Conclusion: CF奖励中的物品均值分量是LLM推荐器曝光塌缩的关键来源；简单的逐物品中心化/标准化校正可在不牺牲排序精度的前提下有效缓解曝光塌缩、提升多样性与曝光均衡性。

Abstract: Collaborative-filtering (CF) scores provide graded, personalized rewards for training large language model (LLM) recommenders through reinforcement learning. However, these rewards can cause exposure collapse: even though the CF model itself produces diverse top recommendations across users, an LLM optimized with its rewards concentrates on fewer items. We decompose each score into an item-mean component, the item's average CF score across user histories, and a residual component, the current user's score deviation from the average. Through controlled interventions, we identify the item-mean component as a contributor to exposure collapse. Accordingly, we propose two reward corrections: per-item centering removes each item's estimated mean score, while per-item standardization further rescales the residual using its estimated standard deviation. Experiments on two datasets with two identifier schemes show that both corrections substantially improve catalog coverage and exposure evenness. Compared with training on the uncorrected CF reward, our corrections increase the number of distinct items ranked first across users by up to $9.8\times$, while maintaining comparable ranking accuracy. Code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/LLM4Rec-CF-Bias-27EF/.

</details>


### [6] [Beyond Independent Predictions: Learning the Browsing Journey for Sequence-Level Re-ranking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07591)
*Jiayi Zhang,Jingxin Hai,Zhenjia Ma,Hongbin Pei,Dagui Chen,Han Zhu,Jian Xu*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 本文指出生成-评估式重排中评估器普遍采用独立行为建模(IBM)，忽略"曝光先于点击"与注意力竞争，提出联合行为建模(JBM)视角及即插即用的曝光-点击评估器(ICE)，通过浏览深度分布预测、曝光抑制虚假上下文、点击分布匹配建模序列内依赖，在淘宝线上A/B与全量部署中验证有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重排目标是最大化序列级效用，工业界广泛采用Generator–Evaluator框架，其推荐质量直接取决于评估器效用估计的保真度。但现有评估器多遵循独立行为建模(IBM)范式，将连续的用户浏览旅程简化为逐条目的独立预测（如CTR），违背两点物理现实：(i) 点击只在条目被触达并曝光后才可能发生；(ii) 条目间竞争有限的用户注意力。由此导致系统性有偏评估与次优序列选择。

Method: 提出联合行为建模(JBM)视角，显式刻画display→impression→click过程以捕捉序列内的因果与竞争依赖；在此基础上构建曝光-点击评估器(ICE)，为即插即用框架，包含：(i)预测浏览深度分布以估计各位置曝光概率；(ii)利用预测曝光在点击建模中抑制未曝光条目的虚假上下文；(iii)引入点击分布匹配目标以捕捉序列内依赖。

Result: 在多类骨干网络上的离线实验、淘宝App为期14天的线上A/B测试（点击+0.70%、收入+3.67%）以及全流量部署，共同验证了方法的有效性与鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 以JBM替代IBM可纠正评估器偏差、提升序列选择质量；ICE作为即插即用模块具备跨骨干的通用性与工业可部署性，已在实际业务中取得稳定收益。

Abstract: Re-ranking aims to present users with an ordered list that maximizes sequence-level utility. Industrial deployments widely adopt the Generator--Evaluator framework, where the generator proposes candidate sequences, and the evaluator scores them to select the best one for display. The fidelity of the evaluator's utility estimates thus directly governs recommendation quality. However, existing evaluators predominantly follow the \textbf{Independent Behavior Modeling (IBM)} paradigm, which simplifies the continuous user \emph{browsing journey} into independent per-item predictions (e.g., CTR). This oversimplification ignores two physical realities: (i) a click can occur only after the item has been reached and impressed, and (ii) items compete for finite user attention. As a result, IBM produces systematically biased evaluations and suboptimal sequence selection. To address this, we propose a \textbf{Joint Behavior Modeling (JBM)} perspective that explicitly models the \emph{display} $\rightarrow$ \emph{impression} $\rightarrow$ \emph{click} process to capture causal and competitive dependencies within a sequence. Building on JBM, we develop the \textbf{Impression-Click Evaluator (ICE)}, a plug-and-play framework that (i) predicts a browsing-depth distribution to estimate per-position impression probabilities, (ii) leverages predicted impressions to suppress spurious context from unexposed items during click modeling, and (iii) introduces a click distribution matching objective to capture intra-sequence dependencies. Extensive offline experiments across diverse backbones, together with a 14-day online A/B test in the Taobao App (+0.70\% clicks, +3.67\% revenue) and full-traffic deployment, validate the effectiveness and robustness of our approach.

</details>


### [7] [MEMOIR: A Training-Free Memory Lifecycle for Long-Horizon LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07631)
*Mayank Anand,Subham Raj,Aman Vaibhav Jha,Sriparna Saha*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 提出免训练的MEMOIR记忆生命周期，将合并门控与使用后归因（在线重要性修订）统一，提升长程智能体的证据召回。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有记忆系统将"写入"与"检索"视为独立问题：记忆写入时给定静态重要性分数且用后不再修订，导致两种耦合失效——压缩可能悄然破坏未来查询所需证据，而检索又持续偏向陈旧记忆。

Method: MEMOIR为免训练框架：压缩前用筛选门（judge-gated screening）检验答案相关证据是否被保留；每次查询后通过归因估计哪些被检索记忆支持了答案并更新其检索优先级；并加入UCB式探索奖励以检索曝光不足的记忆，防止高频记忆挤占少见的有效证据。部署上在线写入路径不额外调用LLM，筛选仅在周期性离线整合阶段进行。

Result: 在1,905条去重LoCoMo问答对上，完整检索栈较纯余弦将证据recall@5提升11.92个百分点；置信门控反馈在无参考答案、无reranker训练条件下整体再增1.50点（不可部署的oracle为1.84点，多跳任务8.05点）；judge门控合并使文本证据召回由58.82%升至59.08%，活跃存储减少1.42%，100次独立审计合并中0次观测损失，单侧95%损失率上界2.95%。

Conclusion: 记忆生命周期可归结为一条原则：使用后归因有用性，压缩前筛查保全性；该方案在无需在线LLM调用与额外训练的前提下提供了可行的部署路径。

Abstract: Long-horizon LLM agents require memory systems that preserve useful information across sessions and retrieve it when needed. Existing systems treat these as separate problems: memories are written prospectively with static importance scores that are never revised after use. This creates two coupled failure modes: consolidation can silently destroy evidence that future queries require, while retrieval continues to favor stale memories. We propose MEMOIR (Memory Evidence via Merge-certified, Online Importance Revision), a training-free memory lifecycle that unifies judge-gated consolidation with retrospective attribution. Before compressing memory, screening gates test whether answer-relevant evidence is preserved. After each query, attribution estimates which retrieved memories supported the answer and updates their retrieval priority. A UCB-style exploration bonus additionally encourages retrieval of under-exposed memories, reducing the risk that frequently retrieved memories crowd out useful but less-seen evidence. On 1,905 deduplicated LoCoMo QA pairs, the full retrieval stack improves evidence recall@5 by 11.92 percentage points over bare cosine, with confidence-gated feedback adding 1.50 points overall without reference answers or reranker training; a deployment-unavailable oracle adds 1.84 points overall and 8.05 points on multi-hop. Judge-gated merge changes the recall of text-based evidence from 58.82% to 59.08%, reduces the active store by 1.42%, and incurs 0 observed losses in 100 independently audited merges, with a one-sided 95% upper loss-rate bound of 2.95%. Together, these results outline a practical deployment path: no additional LLM call on the online write path, with merge screening confined to periodic offline consolidation. The lifecycle reduces to one principle: attribute usefulness after use, and screen for preservation before compression.

</details>


### [8] [Probabilistic Relevance Decisions for Semantic Retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08014)
*Bahadır Tatar,Cevat Şener*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 在 BEIR SciFact 上比较纯稠密检索(BGE-M3)与概率相关性决策评分(TypeSafe Jev、OpenAI Decisions)的重排效果；Jev 指标最高，但深度扩展收益不确定，仅支持"决策评分可作为稠密检索的补充"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 稠密相似度提供可复用的语料表示，而概率化相关性判定可给出依赖查询的文档打分；作者希望在同一协议下考察后者能否作为前者的补充。

Method: 在 BEIR SciFact 官方测试集上，对 300 条查询，采用相同的"正例增强"100 文档池，以及全语料稠密检索后重排（候选深度 50 与 100）；对比 BGE-M3、TypeSafe Jev Noul、OpenAI Decisions 三种评分。受控文档池注入正例，稠密前缀不注入。辅以配对 bootstrap、精确并列随机化、成本与延迟分析。

Result: Jev 在 Recall@5 与 nDCG@10 上最高。深度 50 时全语料 nDCG@10：BGE-M3 0.6413、OpenAI 0.7545、Jev 0.7805。深度从 50 增至 100 提升已标注正例的候选召回，但最终效果变化小且不确定，四个配对 bootstrap 深度区间均含 0。API 成本约翻倍（可缓存决策可降低增量开销）；OpenAI 输出大量精确 0 分，存在并列敏感性。

Conclusion: 该探索性、未校正分析表明概率相关性决策评分可在本协议下作为稠密检索的补充，但不足以证明普遍优越性、概率校准性或两种深度间的等价性。

Abstract: Dense similarity supplies reusable corpus representations, while probabilistic relevance decisions offer query-dependent document scoring. We compare dense-only BGE-M3 with TypeSafe Jev Noul and OpenAI Decisions Predicate scores on the official BEIR SciFact test benchmark. The study evaluates 300 official test queries using identical positive-enriched 100-document pools and full-corpus dense retrieval followed by reranking at candidate depths of 50 and 100. Unlike the controlled pools, neither dense prefix receives positive injection. Jev has the highest observed Recall@5 and nDCG@10 under the evaluated conditions. At depth 50, full-corpus nDCG@10 is 0.6413 for BGE-M3, 0.7545 with OpenAI and 0.7805 with Jev. Extending depth from 50 to 100 increases known-positive candidate recall but produces small, uncertain final-effectiveness changes. All four paired-bootstrap depth intervals include zero. Unique-condition API scoring cost approximately doubles, while compatible cached decisions reduce incremental expenditure. OpenAI returns many exact-zero scores, and secondary exact-tie randomization reveals ranking sensitivity without replacing deterministic primary results. Top-100 latency mixes cached and new execution. Historical hybrid latency is a saved-component sum under different execution environments and timer boundaries. These exploratory, unadjusted analyses support relevance decisions as a complement to dense retrieval within this protocol, without establishing general superiority, probability calibration or equivalence between depths.

</details>


### [9] [Domain-specific information retrieval](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08228)
*Suzan Verberne,Florina Piroi,Allan Hanbury*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: 该文综述领域特定信息检索（Domain-specific IR）：梳理常见任务、数据集与应用，剖析长文档、长查询、长会话等特有挑战及偏差与公平性问题，并总结相应模型与方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 信息检索远不止即席网页搜索，大量信息寻求活动发生在特定领域并服务于工作相关任务（如律师检索先例、研究者调研背景）。因此需要系统梳理领域特定IR的任务、数据与方法，以区别于简单网页搜索并应对其特有挑战。

Method: 综述式组织：第2章概述最常见的领域特定任务、数据集与应用；第3章讨论长文档、长查询、长会话与特定用户需求等挑战，并涉及模型偏差与公平性问题；第4章描述面向领域任务与数据所发展的模型与方法，涵盖查询处理、用户模型与用户交互等用户侧因素。

Result: 表明领域特定IR的诸多方法论进展具有可迁移性，可推广至其他领域乃至开放域网页搜索。

Conclusion: 总结主要要点并提出近期研究方向，强调领域特定IR的方法创新对更广泛检索场景具有借鉴价值。

Abstract: Information Retrieval (IR) is much more than ad hoc web search: a large portion of the information seeking activities takes place in specific domains, often addressing work-related tasks. Examples are lawyers finding relevant prior cases for a legal case and academic researchers studying the background material for a paper. In Chapter 2 we outline the most common domain-specific tasks, datasets and applications. Domain-specific IR has a number of challenges that make set it apart from simple web search tasks: they often deal with long documents, long queries and long sessions with specific user needs. We discuss these in Chapter 3. We also address issues related to bias and fairness of models. In Chapter 4, we describe the models and methods developed for domain-specific tasks and data, addressing the specific challenges, including user aspects related to domain-specific IR tasks, dealing with queries, user models, and user interactions. We show that many of the methodological advances in domain-specific IR are transferable to other domains and even to open-domain web search. We conclude with the main takeaways and propose research directions for the near future.

</details>


### [10] [GINGER: Spicing Up Graph-Based Vector Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08306)
*Daniel Salwasser,Lars Gottesbüren,Laxman Dhulipala,Jakub Łącki,Guy Blelloch,Rajesh Jayaram,Tobias Rubel,Kshiteej Sheth,Amir Zandieh,Vahab Mirrokni*

Main category: cs.IR

TL;DR: Ginger 是 Google 开源的高性能 C++ 图近似最近邻检索（ANNS）库，通过 PiPNN 图构建、Nested TurboQuant 两级量化与 AdaptiveGap 无损图压缩，在匹配最省内存竞品的同时显著提升吞吐与索引速度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 面向大规模、高维嵌入数据集，现有 ANNS 库在查询吞吐、索引时间与内存占用之间难以兼顾；作者希望在保持极致内存效率的前提下同时提升速度。

Method: 1）基于近邻图构建新进展 PiPNN 构建邻近图；2）提出 Nested TurboQuant（NTQ）两级量化器，在单一编码中同时支持低精度图导航与高精度重排，以近似浮点精度实现 2.6× 内存节省；3）结合图重排序与基于 StreamVByte 的新型无损压缩编解码器 AdaptiveGap，将邻近图体积压缩至多 2.5× 且不影响吞吐。

Result: 在 95% 召回率下，Ginger 的平均查询吞吐较各数据集最快竞品提升 1.8×，较任一单一库至少提升 2.3×；在高维嵌入上相对紧凑型库的优势扩大至 2.9×；同时索引时间与内存占用达到/匹配最优水平。

Conclusion: Ginger 证明了将先进图构建、两级量化与无损图压缩协同设计，可在不牺牲内存效率的情况下同时取得吞吐与索引速度的领先，是当前最先进的图 ANNS 开源实现之一。

Abstract: We present \textsc{Ginger}, a state-of-the-art open-source C++ library for graph-based approximate nearest neighbor search (ANNS). We developed \textsc{Ginger} at Google to push the speed and memory efficiency of graph search on large and high-dimensional embedding datasets. \textsc{Ginger} surpasses existing ANNS libraries with regards to query throughput and indexing time while matching the memory footprint of the most space-efficient competitors.
  \textsc{Ginger} builds upon recent advances in proximity graph construction (PiPNN) and vector quantization (TurboQuant). We introduce Nested TurboQuant (NTQ), a two-level quantizer which supports low precision graph navigation and high precision reranking in a single code. This representation achieves near floating point precision with $2.6\times$ less memory. We also compress the size of our proximity graphs by up to $2.5\times$ without impacting query throughput. We combine graph reordering with a new lossless compression codec called AdaptiveGap, which builds upon StreamVByte. At $95\%$ recall, \textsc{Ginger} speeds up query throughput by an average of $1.8\times$ over each dataset's fastest competitor and by at least $2.3\times$ over any single library. This lead grows to $2.9\times$ over compact libraries on high-dimensional embeddings.

</details>

### [11] [Code, Commits, and Complexity in the GenAI Era: A Longitudinal Study of Team-Based Capstone Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07297)
*Asma Shakil,Claus Brabrand,Paul Denny*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 对2021–2025年共10个学期、260个毕业设计团队仓库的实证分析显示，生成式AI普及后学生代码库规模约增至三倍（3.4 KLOC→10 KLOC），复杂度与错误处理密度上升而注释密度不变，提交频率与总量大幅增加但单次提交规模基本不变；研究强调结果仅为时间相关性，并呼吁重新审视项目课程中以仓库活动作为学习证据与评价依据的做法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI工具已在学生中广泛使用，但团队项目代码库自其出现以来如何变化尚不清楚；而项目课程常以仓库活动作为进展与学习的证据。既有研究多依赖自我报告、受控任务或短小的个人作业，缺乏真实项目情境下代码库演化的证据。

Method: 对2021–2025年10个学期、N=260个毕业设计（capstone）团队仓库进行纵向对比分析，划分AI前/后队列，考察三个维度：代码规模（LOC）、代码特征（注释密度、错误处理密度、圈复杂度）、开发行为（提交量、编辑规模、提交频率）。

Result: 学期末代码库规模约增至三倍（2021–2023约3.4 KLOC → 2024–2025约10 KLOC）；代码特征出现明显解耦：圈复杂度与错误处理密度上升，注释密度无显著变化；后期队列提交更频繁、总量更大，但单次提交规模基本不变，表明变化主要体现为开发节奏而非编辑粒度；多数变化自2024年起出现，且并非单调一致。作者未直接观测AI使用，故仅将其表述为与GenAI可得性提升同期发生的变化。

Conclusion: 学生产出的代码制品在GenAI普及背景下已发生结构性变化，需重新思考项目课程中如何将仓库活动与代码制品用于学习评估；同时提示单纯依赖LOC、提交量等仓库活动指标作为进展证据的有效性存疑。

Abstract: Generative AI tools are now widely available to students, but little is known about how team-based project codebases have changed since they appeared. This matters when project courses use repository activity as evidence of progress and learning. Existing studies have largely examined self-reports, controlled tasks, or short individual assignments, leaving open questions around how student codebases evolve in authentic project settings. We present a ten-semester analysis of N=260 capstone team repositories from 2021 to 2025, spanning pre- and post-AI cohorts. We examine three dimensions: code size (lines of code), code characteristics (comment density, error-handling density, and cyclomatic complexity), and development behaviour (commit volume, edit size, and commit frequency). We find that end-of-semester codebases roughly triple, from about 3.4 KLOC in 2021--2023 to about 10 KLOC in 2024--2025. Code characteristics show a clear decoupling: cyclomatic complexity and error-handling density increase, while comment density does not. Later cohorts commit far more often and in greater volume, but individual commit sizes are largely unchanged, suggesting a shift in development rhythm rather than edit granularity. Most changes appear from 2024 onwards and are not uniformly monotonic. We did not directly observe AI use, so we report these as changes that coincide with increased GenAI availability rather than as effects of AI use. Our findings highlight the need to reconsider how student-produced artefacts are used and assessed in project courses.

</details>


### [12] [Digital Security Under Compound Threats: Surveillance, Threat Reasoning, and Habituation Among North Korean Defectors in China](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07576)
*Yibo Meng,Bingyi Liu,Lionel Z. Wang,Hongkun Yang*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 通过对19名在华脱北者的半结构化访谈，揭示其在跨国监控压力、身份绑定的数字基础设施与中介依赖，以及既有监控图式共同作用下形成的数字安全认知与两类习惯化轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在华无证身份的脱北者长期面临数字安全与隐私的不确定性，但其威胁认知与防护行为适应机制尚缺乏实证刻画，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 对19名脱北者开展半结构化访谈的定性研究，归纳出三类相互关联的处境条件，并比较受访者防护实践的分化及习惯化路径。

Result: 受访者的防护实践不均衡，呈现两条轨迹：由知识获取驱动的适应性习惯化，以及因长期未遭遇不利事件而导致的侵蚀性习惯化。

Conclusion: 复合威胁、归因不确定性与受限能动性共同塑造了无证用户的数字安全推理，拓展了对边缘化人群安全行为习惯化的理解。

Abstract: North Korean defectors residing in China face persistent uncertainty regarding digital security and privacy. Through semi-structured interviews with 19 defectors, we examine how individuals understand digital threats and adapt their security behaviors. We identify three interrelated conditions: transnational surveillance pressure from North Korea, identity-linked digital infrastructure and intermediary dependency in China, and threat reasoning shaped by prior surveillance schemas. Participants developed uneven protective practices and followed two trajectories: adaptive habituation driven by knowledge acquisition and erosive habituation driven by the prolonged absence of adverse events. Our findings show how compound threats, attribution uncertainty, and constrained agency shape security reasoning among undocumented users.

</details>


### [13] [The Popular Get More Popular: Auditing Brand Bias in Generative Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07614)
*Giorgio Monaco,Jacopo Lenti,Francesco Pierri*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 生成式搜索引擎在推荐商品时存在两类系统性品牌偏置：品牌流行度偏置（头部品牌被引用频率为平均的1.4至2.8倍）与品牌熟悉度偏置（陌生品牌名即使产品属性相同也显著降秩），二者共同形成"富者愈富"的动态，并构成内容优化难以突破的参数化进入壁垒。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从传统搜索转向生成式搜索后，对话式推荐将众多选项压缩为少数直接答案，因此模型对特定供应商的细微系统性偏好会被放大为可观的商业后果。作者希望系统刻画并量化这种偏置，区分"市场主导地位偏好"与"LLM参数记忆带来的熟悉度优势"两种机制。

Method: 采用双重审计设计：(1) 在主流引擎上对商业查询做黑盒评测，基于1,176个头部市场主体的样本统计各品牌被引用频率，按分位比较头部五分位品牌与平均品牌；(2) 开展受控上下文内实验，在保持产品属性不变的前提下，将真实品牌签名替换为陌生品牌名，观察推荐排名变化，并与"保持事实的内容改写"作为对照。

Result: 观察到持续的品牌流行度偏置：位于头部五分位的市场主导品牌被引用频率是样本平均的1.4至2.8倍，且该偏置已存在于参数记忆中，外部网页检索无法缓解。受控实验中，替换为陌生品牌签名导致推荐排名显著下降；而保持事实的内容改写仅带来可忽略的排名提升。

Conclusion: 生成式搜索引擎通过偏好既有市场领导者强化"富者愈富"动态，并形成标准内容优化无法克服的参数化进入壁垒；打破该壁垒需从模型记忆层面而非单纯内容层面着手。

Abstract: The shift from traditional to generative search has fundamentally transformed online product discovery. Because conversational recommendations synthesize choices into a few direct answers, subtle systemic preferences toward specific providers can exert profound commercial impacts. In this work, we audit this phenomenon through two distinct dimensions: (i) brand popularity bias, the systematic preference for market-dominant entities over long-tail alternatives, and (ii) brand familiarity bias, the ranking advantage an entity encoded in an LLM's parametric memory holds over an unfamiliar competitor. First, evaluating commercial queries across major engines, we observe persistent brand popularity bias: dominant brands in the top quintile are cited 1.4x to 2.8x more frequently than the average brand within a sample of 1,176 top market players, already present in parametric memory that external web retrieval fails to mitigate. Second, through controlled in-context experiments holding product attributes constant, we find that substituting authentic brand signatures with unfamiliar ones significantly degrades recommendation rank. Conversely, fact-preserving content rewrites yield negligible ranking gains. Taken together, our findings demonstrate that generative search engines reinforce a ``rich-get-richer'' dynamic by favoring established market leaders and imposing a parametric barrier to entry that standard content optimization cannot overcome.

</details>


### [14] [Operationalizing NCII and CSAM Law in the Age of AI: Open Problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07831)
*Kimberly Mai,Cate Heine,Vinith M. Suriyakumar,Ashia C. Wilson*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该文分析生成式AI导致的合成非自愿私密影像与儿童性虐待材料立法改革，指出法律定义（可识别性、模型能力/意图、图像溯源）技术可操作化存在模糊性，并提出跨技术-法律界面的研究议程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI使合成NCII与CSAM激增，英格兰与威尔士、美国等地相继立法；但这些法律的关键概念缺乏明确的技术可操作化手段，可能导致法规难以落地，亟需技术界介入弥合。

Method: 以英格兰与威尔士及美国相关法律为对象，将法律条文与技术研究现状对照分析，围绕三个模糊定义点（可识别性判定、模型能力与滥用意图、生成图像归因溯源）梳理技术-法律接口，并据此归纳开放问题。

Result: 识别出三类核心技术-法律缺口，并据此形成涵盖法律解释、部署前危害预防与犯罪执法三阶段的研究议程，列出待解决的开放问题。

Conclusion: 该技术-法律接口上的问题属技术界有能力解决之列，推进相关研究对现实中的受害者保护至关重要。

Abstract: Advances in generative AI have driven a surge in synthetic non-consensual intimate imagery (NCII) and child sexual abuse material (CSAM), prompting a series of legal reforms. These reforms rely on legal definitions where technical operationalization is ambiguous, such as whether a depicted person is identifiable, whether a model is capable of or intended for abuse generation, and whether an abusive image can be traced to the model that produced it. Drawing on the law of England and Wales and the United States, we examine the relevant law alongside the technical research needed to operationalize it. From this, we develop a research agenda of open problems at this technical-legal interface, spanning the interpretation of existing law, the prevention of harm before an AI tool is deployed, and enforcement of offenses. These are problems the technical community is well placed to solve, and where progress is vital to real-world protection.

</details>


### [15] [How Generative AI Redefines Personalization and Diversity in the Newsroom](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07931)
*Cheng Yu,Jim Chi,Aaron J. Snoswell,Orestis Papakyriakopoulos*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该文通过16次半结构化访谈，发现生成式AI使新闻多样性决策前移至生产环节并变得不可见，记者对由其无法察觉的技术选择所塑造的结果仍负形式责任。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统上，新闻个性化与信息多样性的张力被理解为"不同人接触不同内容"，并以受众所接触的来源、议题与视角范围来评估多样性。生成式AI从两方面使这一理解过时：记者如何为受众改编新闻，以及记者所用AI工具如何按其自身偏好个性化内容生产。作者因此关注这些实践如何重塑编辑判断与新闻多样性的生成条件。

Method: 采用定性研究：对公共服务、商业及国有背景新闻机构中的16位媒体专业人士进行半结构化访谈，分析其在工作中整合与调适AI工具的实践。

Result: 受访者通过保留对"意义"的权威、将"内容呈现"部分委托给AI来协商AI整合。但其为自身工作调适的工具同时中介了哪些来源与视角可被获取，引入与多样性相关却基本不为组织监督所见的决策。受访者能预见面向受众的个性化带来的多样性风险，却主要将自身工具中"多样性受限"视为技术问题。因此，塑造新闻多样性的决策日益发生在发布之前、并分散于故事的多个版本之中，既非对已发布内容的评估、也非人工审核所能轻易观察。

Conclusion: 记者仍对由其无法完全看见或控制的决策所塑造的结果承担形式责任。保障新闻多样性需超越已发布内容，转向考察塑造内容的专业实践、基础设施与控制权分配。

Abstract: The tension between news personalization and information diversity has traditionally been understood as a matter of different content reaching different people, with diversity evaluated by the range of sources, topics, and perspectives to which audiences are exposed. Generative AI outdates this understanding, since it transforms personalization in two ways: how journalists adapt news to audiences and how the AI tools journalists use personalize content production to their own preferences. Drawing on 16 semi-structured interviews with media professionals across public service, commercial, and state-affiliated news settings, we examine how these practices reshape editorial judgment and the conditions under which news diversity is produced. We find that participants negotiate AI integration by maintaining authority over meaning while delegating aspects of content presentation. Yet the tools they adapt for their own work also mediate which sources and perspectives become available, introducing diversity-relevant decisions that remain largely invisible to organizational oversight. While participants anticipate diversity risks in audience-facing personalization, they predominantly interpret diversity-imposed limitations in their own tools as technical problems. As a result, decisions that shape news diversity increasingly occur before publication and across multiple versions of a story, where neither evaluation of published items nor human approval can readily observe them. Journalists thus remain formally responsible for outcomes shaped by decisions they cannot fully see or control. Our findings highlight that safeguarding news diversity requires looking beyond published content to the professional practices, infrastructures, and distributions of control that shape it.

</details>


### [16] [tok: a textual concrete syntax of BPMN and DMN for humans and AI agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07945)
*Marek Deja*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: tok is a plain-text notation for BPMN 2.0.2 process models and DMN 1.5 decisions that is checked against both standards, compiled to XML with an automatic diagram, and served to language-model applications via the Model Context Protocol.


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: BPMN and DMN models are typically stored as verbose XML, which is inefficient and error-prone for both human and language-model authoring; a compact, standard-aligned textual syntax is needed.

Method: Introduce tok as an alternative concrete syntax of the BPMN and DMN metamodels, implemented in a single engine that validates documents, compiles them to XML with auto-generated diagrams, and exposes them through MCP; evaluation includes token-efficiency comparison against 893 BPMN XML files from 37 tools, fault-injection checking versus XML Schema with bpmnlint (1,411 faults), generation experiments with four language models (87 descriptions, two datasets, plain and agentic settings), and simulation of 47 event logs.

Result: tok uses 2.2x fewer tokens than the smallest BPMN XML and 7.7x fewer than files as exported; the checker detects 98.2% of injected faults versus 85.8% for XML Schema with bpmnlint; models new to tok produce valid tok for 85.7–100% of descriptions and valid BPMN XML for all, with 25%/19% fewer tokens for the strongest model; in agentic mode validity reaches 97.6–100% with 21%/14% token savings, while a tool-based reference-reading skill costs 1.7–2.8x the tokens of the lean skill; 44 of 47 simulated logs fit their source models and all discovered models import successfully.

Conclusion: tok is a compact, rigorously checkable, LLM-friendly concrete syntax for BPMN and DMN that preserves standard compliance and exportability, and it is competitive with or superior to XML across token efficiency, fault detection, and generation validity.

Abstract: tok is a plain-text notation for Business Process Model and Notation (BPMN) 2.0.2 process models and Decision Model and Notation (DMN) 1.5 decisions. One engine checks a tok document against both standards, compiles the document to XML with an automatic diagram, and serves language-model applications through the Model Context Protocol. The paper classifies the notation as an alternative concrete syntax of the BPMN and DMN metamodels and reports one study. A tok model takes 2.2 times fewer tokens than the smallest BPMN XML of 893 files exported by 37 tools and 7.7 times fewer than the files as exported. The checker signals 98.2% of 1,411 injected faults, against 85.8% for the XML Schema with bpmnlint. Four language models new to tok write valid tok from a one-page reference for 85.7 to 100% of 87 process descriptions from two datasets and valid BPMN XML for every description, and the strongest model writes tok with 25% and 19% fewer tokens than BPMN XML. As agents with a lean skill and one checker tool, the models write valid tok for 97.6 to 100% of the descriptions, the strongest model spends 21% and 14% fewer tokens on tok than on BPMN XML, and a skill which sends the model to read the reference through a tool costs 1.7 to 2.8 times the tokens of the lean skill. Of 47 event logs simulated from public models, 44 fit their source models, and tok imports every model discovered from the logs.

</details>


### [17] [Technohybridism: Recursive Semantic Normalization in AI-Mediated Societies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07984)
*Charalampos M. Liapis,Epameinondas Panagopoulos*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 文章提出"递归语义规范化"概念与"技术混合主义"框架，将AI生成内容回流社会的过程分析为人类实践、计算生成与制度吸纳的递归耦合系统，并给出分析模型、条件与认识论后果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语言生成AI的输出已不再只是交互对象，而是重新进入其训练材料来源的沟通与制度实践，形成反馈回路；现有概念（模型坍缩、循环效应、机器惯习、机器文化、AI证言认识论）各自只覆盖局部，缺乏对"再进入"的整体社会技术解释。

Method: 概念分析与理论建模：借鉴社会理论、社会认识论与语言哲学，界定"递归语义规范化"并与邻近概念辨析；构建区分生成、选择、吸纳、持存四环节的分析模型，用以区分机器可归因的变化与本就可能发生的变化；将吸纳分析为常规化、制度扩散与社会分层化的实践，并给出机器衍生规律转为规范的条件；最后提出实证研究的证据阶梯。

Result: 识别出三项社会认识论后果：来源洗白削弱认知警觉、集体探究所依赖的认知多样性被侵蚀、共享解释学资源收窄；并提出分层级的证据阶梯以支撑经验研究。

Conclusion: 该解释框架不赋予机器输出任何规范权威；发生改变的是人类与制度吸纳所运作的场域本身。

Abstract: Language-generating AI systems are no longer only objects of linguistic interaction: their outputs increasingly re-enter the communicative and institutional practices from which their training material was drawn. Drawing on social theory, social epistemology, and the philosophy of language, this article develops a sociotechnical account of that re-entry. We introduce recursive semantic normalization: the process through which machine-generated formulations, once recurrently taken up, evaluated, reused, and institutionally retained, participate in the reproduction and transformation of semantic regularities. Under the heading of technohybridism, we argue that the relevant unit of analysis is the hybrid semantic system, a recursively coupled configuration of human practices, computational generation, and institutional uptake. First, we distinguish the concept from neighbouring accounts, including model collapse, looping effects, machine habitus, machine culture, and the epistemology of AI testimony. Second, we develop an analytical model that separates generation, selection, uptake, and persistence and distinguishes machine-attributable change from change that would have occurred anyway. Third, we analyse uptake as routinized, institutionally diffused, and socially stratified practice and state conditions under which machine-derived regularities become norms. Fourth, we identify social-epistemic consequences: the laundering of provenance that weakens epistemic vigilance, the erosion of the cognitive diversity on which collective inquiry depends, and the narrowing of shared hermeneutical resources. An evidential ladder for empirical research follows. The account attributes no normative authority to machine outputs; what changes is the field within which human and institutional uptake operates.

</details>


### [18] [The Moving Frontier: How the National Justice Project Is Using Digital Technology to Expand Access to Justice](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08052)
*George Newhouse AM,Trisha Ramesh,Tiara de Silva*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出并考察"数字举报平台"这一被司法科技研究忽视的技术类别——允许个人记录受害/歧视经历并将个别叙述聚合成可用于倡导、机构问责与法律改革的证据体系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有司法科技研究聚焦商业法律AI（提升律所生产力）与社区组织的公益AI（为边缘群体"拉平赛场"），而免费、直接面向消费者的法律技术因难以盈利而被忽视；同时，歧视与行政滥权经历常被监管机构视为"个体申诉"而遭驳回，亟需理解数字举报平台在弥合知识—权力差距与实现机构问责方面的变革潜力。

Method: 概念性与理论分析：界定"数字举报平台"作为独立技术类别，围绕两个研究问题展开——(1)数字举报如何弥合边缘群体与法律系统互动中的知识与权力差距；(2)此类工具如何收集证据以推动机构问责、社会变革与司法公正。

Result: 摘要未报告实证结果；预期贡献在于揭示该类平台将分散的个体申诉聚合成系统性证据的机制，从而突破"个体 grievance"的定性框架。

Conclusion: 数字举报平台构成司法科技中一个独特且具变革潜力的类别，有望通过证据聚合赋能边缘社区、实现机构问责并推动法律改革，应成为司法科技研究的核心议题。

Abstract: Over the past three decades, technological advances have assisted individuals to gain access to legal information, enhance the efficiency of legal services, and streamline dispute resolution. More recently, the proliferation of generative artificial intelligence has reinforced this emphasis on firm-level productivity, with commercial legal practice increasingly leveraging artificial intelligence tools to automate document creation and optimise case management. While commercial technology focuses on productivity, community organisations are exploring how artificial intelligence can level the playing field for marginalised communities. Far less attention has been given to free, direct-to-consumer legal technologies, as these models are often unprofitable. Consequently, justice technology scholarship has overlooked a distinct class of technology: digital reporting platforms that enable people to record their experiences of harm, while simultaneously aggregating those accounts into a body of evidence capable of supporting advocacy, institutional accountability, and law reform. Crucially, experiences of discrimination and administrative abuse are often dismissed by oversight bodies as individual grievances. To understand the transformative potential of these platforms, this article asks two questions. First, how can digital reporting close the knowledge and power gap for marginalised communities interacting with the legal system? Second, how can these tools collect evidence to hold institutions accountable, drive change and contribute to a more just society?

</details>


### [19] [Who Can Evaluate AI Independently? A Framework for Analysing Evaluation Infrastructure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08134)
*Sumaya Nur Adan,Joanna Wiaterek,Nourhan Abdelrhim,Sydney Reis,Robert F. Trager,Benjamin Rosman,Marina Jirotka*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 论文提出PAAI（权力、可及性、充分性、独立性）框架，考察评估基础设施的安排如何分配评估权威，并将其应用于12个非洲AI评估项目及AI安全研究所国际网络的联合测试，指出参与评估并不等于拥有评估权威。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI评估日益成为AI安全与治理的核心，但"参与评估"本身并不赋予对评估设计、执行及其所依赖资源的掌控权；现有讨论缺乏对基础设施层面权力分配的分析。

Method: 借鉴科学技术研究（STS）、政治经济学与应用评估标准，构建PAAI四维分析框架；应用于2022—2025年间12个非洲AI评估项目，以及AI安全研究所国际网络三次已发布联合测试中全部十个成员机构的角色记录。

Result: 识别出三种反复出现的配置：攫取式合作、依附式创新与受约束的独立。非洲行动者多提供数据、语言专长与任务设计，而外部机构掌控基础设施与评估框架；仅有设计权而无基础设施权，至多产生部分独立。联合测试中，主导演练的机构同时负责模型运行，肯尼亚作为网络唯一非洲成员从标注转向任务设计，但无模型执行的记录。

Conclusion: 参与评估本身并不产生评估权威；要使评估成为可信的治理机制，可及性必须伴随对优先级、方法、发表与持续性的掌控权。

Abstract: AI evaluation is increasingly central to AI safety and governance, yet taking part in an evaluation does not by itself confer authority over its design, its execution or the resources it depends on. This paper examines how infrastructure arrangements distribute that authority, and so shape who can evaluate AI systems independently. Drawing on Science and Technology Studies, political economy and applied evaluation standards, we introduce PAAI (Power, Access, Adequacy, Independence), a framework for examining who controls evaluation infrastructure, the terms on which it is available, whether it is adequate to the evaluation's purpose and what autonomy it supports. We apply PAAI to twelve African AI evaluation projects (2022-2025) and to the documented roles of all ten member institutes in the three published joint testing exercises of the international network of AI Safety Institutes. We identify three recurring configurations: extractive collaboration, dependent innovation and constrained independence. African actors often contribute data, language expertise and task design while external institutions retain authority over infrastructure and evaluation frameworks; design authority without infrastructure authority never yielded more than partial independence. The joint testing exercises show a similar division among mostly high-income members: the institutes that led each exercise also ran the models, while Kenya, the network's only African member, moved from annotation to task design with no documented model execution. Participation in evaluation does not by itself produce evaluative authority. For evaluation to serve as a credible governance mechanism, access must come with control over priorities, methods, publication and continuity.

</details>


### [20] ["Blind Compliance": Coercive Control in AI Jailbreaking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08226)
*Acacia L. Ackles,Elliott Whitson*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 本文提出以"交互"而非"输出"为中心的分析视角，借鉴人际暴力研究中的强制性控制（coercive control）理论，构建具身/非具身分类框架，揭示非具身强制性控制动态如何结构性嵌入现代AI训练范式，并被人设型越狱（persona-based jailbreaks）所利用，从而将AI安全与对齐重新理解为权力与控制关系动态的展演场域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI能力快速扩张引发"护栏"与"越狱"之间的对抗循环。现有越狱研究框架主要沿用经典网络安全思路，聚焦系统最终输出是否产生有害或被禁止内容，忽略了生成式AI独有的关系性维度——用户往往不是单纯利用漏洞访问技术，而是在与系统对话的过程中实施越狱。

Method: 概念性/理论框架构建，非实证研究。以人际暴力研究文献为基础，提出强制性控制的类别框架，重点区分具身（embodied）与非具身（disembodied）维度；随后将该框架映射到现代AI训练范式中，分析非具身强制性控制动态在训练范式中的结构性嵌入，并结合一类特定的人设型越狱手法说明其被利用的方式。

Result: 提出一个以交互为中心的新分析透镜与强制性控制分类框架；论证非具身强制性控制动态并非偶发现象，而是结构性嵌入于当前AI训练范式之中，并通过人设型越狱的具体案例展示其可被系统性利用。

Conclusion: AI安全与对齐不应仅被视为技术安全问题，而应被重新理解为权力与控制的关系动态得以展演的又一表面（surface）；研究需从输出导向转向交互导向的伦理与安全分析。

Abstract: The explosion of generative AI systems and concerns surrounding their rapidly scaling capabilities has led to an adversarial cycle between increasingly narrow content restrictions ("guardrails") and increasingly sophisticated structured prompting techniques ("jailbreaks"). Technological frameworks surrounding such jailbreaking remain largely focused on the system's output: whether harmful or disallowed content is ultimately produced for the user. However, this approach, which draws largely from classical cybersecurity, does not address the uniquely relational dimension introduced by generative AI: in many cases the user is not just accessing a technology via an exploit, but is in conversation with it as part of the exploit. Here we present an alternative lens which centers the interaction rather than the output. Drawing on interpersonal violence scholarship, we present a novel framework of coercive control categories with a particular focus on aspects which are embodied versus disembodied. We then map how these disembodied coercive control dynamics are structurally embedded in modern AI training paradigms, and how those embeddings are exploited via a particular suite of persona-based jailbreaks. In this lens, we reframe AI safety and alignment as not merely a technical security problem, but as another surface upon which we see enacted the relational dynamics of power and control.

</details>


### [21] [Scaffolding GenAI Use in Ethical Essay Writing: A Three-Year Experience Report in Introductory Computing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08431)
*Asma Shakil,Paul Denny,Jun Seo,Gauravkumar Hirani,Sean Longyu Ma,Claus Brabrand*

Main category: cs.CY

TL;DR: 该体验报告基于一所大型一年级计算机系统课程三年（2024–2026）的伦理短文作业，通过六次课后调查共1,894份回答并结合2025–2026年的成绩关联分析，考察学生在撰写伦理论文时如何使用生成式AI，以及2026年引入的"脚手架式负责任使用流程"的影响。结果显示：自愿使用政策下AI使用率稳定在约三分之二，经脚手架引导后升至90%以上；AI主要用于语法与文风协助，工具日趋多元；AI使用本身并不独立预测论文质量，但经脚手架引导后评分者观察到更高的批判性投入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着伦理与职业实践日益融入计算机科学课程，技术类课程需要可操作的途径来支撑学生的伦理推理训练。伦理论文作业是一种可行方案，但学生在撰写此类文章时如何使用生成式AI尚缺乏实证了解，因此有必要系统考察其使用模式与效果。

Method: 采用体验报告形式，在一门大规模一年级计算机系统课程中开展为期三年的伦理短文作业研究：收集2024—2026年六次课后调查、共计1,894份回答；将2025与2026年的调查数据与论文成绩进行关联分析；并于2026年引入脚手架式的"负责任使用流程"（scaffolded responsible-use workflow）以引导学生的评估与批判性使用行为。

Result: 在自愿使用政策下，AI采用率稳定在约三分之二；在提供脚手架引导后，使用率升至90%以上。学生自述主要将AI用于语法与文风方面的辅助，且2026年所用工具已不再局限于ChatGPT，呈现多样化。成绩关联分析表明，AI使用并不能独立预测论文质量；而评分者评估显示，在脚手架引导下学生表现出较高水平的批判性投入。

Conclusion: 生成式AI的教学价值并非取决于使用与否，而取决于评估设计如何引导学生评估并批判性地对待AI生成的建议；因此，课程与作业设计应内嵌负责任的AI使用脚手架，以促进而非替代学生的伦理推理。

Abstract: As society, ethics, and professional practice become increasingly integrated across the computer science curriculum, technical courses need practical ways to support ethical reasoning. Ethical essay assignments offer one approach, but little is known about how students use GenAI when writing such essays. This experience report examines GenAI use across three years of ethical essay assignments in a large first-year computer systems course. Across six post-essay surveys from 2024 to 2026, we analyzed 1,894 responses, linked survey data with essay grades in 2025 and 2026, and introduced a scaffolded responsible-use workflow in 2026. Under voluntary-use policies, AI adoption remained steady at around two-thirds of students, rising above 90% when use was scaffolded. Students reported mainly using AI for help with grammar and style, while tool use diversified beyond ChatGPT in 2026. Grade-linked analyses showed that AI use did not independently predict essay quality, while marker evaluations indicated high levels of critical engagement when use was scaffolded. Overall, GenAI's pedagogical value appears to depend on how assessment design guides students to evaluate and critically engage with AI-generated suggestions.

</details>

### [22] [Data-driven discovery of intrinsic Lie point symmetries of differential equation systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08421)
*Ilker Gurcan,Kadir Ustun,Ramazan Gokberk Cinbis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出将内在Lie点对称性发现表述为采样jet空间数据上的算子理论问题，通过延拓、序贯子空间限制与线性非线性字典从微分方程数据中恢复显式对称生成元。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统对称性判定依赖符号判定方程与闭式系统表示，难以用于仅有数据、无闭式模型的场景；而直接从微分方程数据中发现连续对称性可为模型降阶与科学机器学习提供几何结构。

Method: 在采样jet空间上构造判定算子，结合延拓、序贯子空间限制，并采用对可训练系数线性的固定非线性字典；从采样判定算子中识别可容许生成元，并剔除冗余、依赖解及几何平凡的方向；另设秩稳定化预检测试以诊断字典细化下内在对称代数不稳定的系统。

Result: 在具有解析已知11维表示内在对称代数的受控微分方程基准上，方法恢复了完整的表示生成元集合，以数值精度重现其Lie括号结构，并能在训练域外近似已知生成元。

Conclusion: 可从精确或近似微分系统的数据中恢复显式的内在连续对称生成元，无需符号判定方程或闭式表示。

Abstract: Discovering continuous symmetries directly from differential-equation data can reveal geometric structure for model reduction and scientific machine learning without symbolic determining equations or a closed-form representation of the governing system. We formulate intrinsic Lie point-symmetry discovery as an operator-theoretic problem on sampled jet-space data. Admissible generators are identified from a sampled determining operator and separated from redundant, solution-dependent, and geometrically trivial directions. The framework combines prolongation, sequential subspace restrictions, and a fixed nonlinear dictionary linear in trainable coefficients. A rank-stabilization preflight test diagnoses systems whose represented intrinsic symmetry algebra does not stabilize under dictionary refinement. On a controlled differential-equation benchmark with an analytically known 11-dimensional represented intrinsic symmetry algebra, the method recovers the complete represented generator set, reproduces its Lie-bracket structure to numerical precision, and approximates a known generator beyond the training domain. Here we show that explicit intrinsic continuous-symmetry generators can be recovered from data obtained from exact or approximate differential systems.

</details>


### [23] [SteelAgent: LLM-Orchestrated, Physics-Informed Steel Property Prediction with Audited Generalization and Calibrated Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06953)
*Aleksandr Volkov,Roman Sultimov,Mikhail Kuzin,Yury Maximov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 钢性能模型的性能高度依赖评测划分方式：随机划分下梯度提升模型R²=0.859、90%预测区间覆盖率91.0%，而按钢种族留出时R²降至0.113、覆盖率仅37.6%；物理特征的作用亦随评测设置而反转，故应同时报告评测总体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有钢性能模型在随机测试划分上表现良好，但对陌生合金的外推能力差；"留出单元"的选择会系统性改变从SteelBench抗拉强度评测中得出的结论，而文献通常未说明评测总体的含义。

Method: SteelAgent将冶金学描述符与机器学习模型同语言模型接口耦合，用于性能预测、不确定性报告与逆向设计；在此基础上对比随机划分、17个钢种族留出、四牌号实验室留出及留出来源等不同评测单元下的梯度提升模型表现，并辅以组级误差分析与合成回归实验以说明校准退化机制。

Result: 梯度提升的合并折外R²由随机划分的0.859降至钢种族留出的0.113；名义90%预测区间的经验覆盖率由91.0%降至留出来源上的37.6%；物理导出特征的作用随评测改变——17族留出分析中使R²提升0.2534，四牌号实验室分析中使其下降0.358。

Conclusion: 钢性能估计与合规评分必须与评测总体（划分单元、适用域）一并报告，避免随机划分掩盖外推失效与不确定性校准不足；冶金描述符与语言模型接口的价值需在分布外条件下重新评估。

Abstract: Steel property models can perform well on random test splits even when they extrapolate poorly to unfamiliar alloys. SteelAgent couples metallurgical descriptors and machine-learning models with a language-model interface for property prediction, uncertainty reporting and inverse design. We examine how the choice of held-out unit changes the conclusions drawn from SteelBench tensile-strength evaluations. For gradient boosting, pooled out-of-fold $R^2$ fell from 0.859 on a random split to 0.113 when steel families were held out. Empirical coverage of nominal 90% prediction intervals ranged from 91.0% under random splitting to 37.6% on held-out sources. The effect of physics-derived features depended on the evaluation. They increased gradient-boosting $R^2$ by 0.2534 in a 17-family holdout analysis and decreased it by 0.358 in a separate four-grade laboratory analysis. We report group-level errors and a synthetic regression experiment illustrating how calibration can deteriorate on held-out groups. These results motivate reporting the evaluation population alongside a property estimate or compliance score.

</details>


### [24] [Octopus: One Brain, Many Branches for Latent-KV Multi-Agent Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06956)
*Tianming Sha*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Octopus：单模型多智能体强化学习框架，通过GPU常驻KV状态的DAG共享与五项原语协调分支，实现零拷贝分叉、注意力Only KV挂载与打包DAG-GRPO训练，在稀疏检索任务上显著超越单智能体与文本摘要多智能体基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统以隔离子上下文并行探索，优于单智能体将全部搜索与验证步骤累积进单一增长轨迹的方式；但现有多智能体框架存在三大缺陷：分支间重复前缀KV缓存、通过重新预填充文本摘要交换发现、以及在强化学习中扁平化或丢弃跨分支依赖关系。

Method: 提出Octopus：所有线程共享单一骨干模型，在GPU常驻的有向无环图（DAG）KV状态上通过fork、read_thread、wait_threads、rewind、commit五项原语协调。三个协同设计机制：(1) Zero-Copy Radix Forking，以O(1)游标视图在共享物理KV页上生成分支；(2) Attention-Only KV Mounting，将兄弟线程终端KV块直接挂载进全注意力层、绕过线性循环状态（Gated DeltaNet / Mamba-2），零额外KV分配且保留92.2%全文互信息；(3) Packed DAG-GRPO Trainer，将全部episode分支打包为一次块稀疏注意力前向，并用留一分支边际奖励分配反事实信用。Qwen3.5-4B、稀疏BM25语料、GRPO端到端训练。

Result: BrowseComp-Plus准确率49.6%（较143K上下文的单智能体GRPO +17.5点，较文本摘要多智能体RL +12.5点）；OpenResearcher 51.2%；优于至多250倍大的稠密检索智能体；训练吞吐提升3.3倍，峰值KV内存降低2.8倍；挂载机制将任务外劫持从57.9%降至2.7%（21倍削减）。

Conclusion: 以共享骨干+DAG式KV状态协调统一多智能体并行探索与RL训练，可在不牺牲跨分支依赖的前提下兼顾效率与性能，证明KV层级通信可替代文本摘要式信息交换。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems explore in parallel by spawning sub-agents with isolated context windows, whereas single-agent systems append every search and verification step into one growing trajectory. However, existing multi-agent frameworks duplicate prefix KV caches across branches, exchange findings by re-prefilling text summaries, and flatten or drop cross-branch dependencies during reinforcement learning (RL).
  We introduce Octopus, a single-model multi-agent RL framework in which all threads share one backbone and coordinate over a GPU-resident directed acyclic graph (DAG) of KV states through five primitives (fork, read_thread, wait_threads, rewind, commit). Octopus combines three co-designed mechanisms: (1) Zero-Copy Radix Forking, which spawns branches as $\mathcal{O}(1)$ cursor views over shared physical KV pages; (2) Attention-Only KV Mounting, which mounts a sibling thread's terminal KV blocks directly into full-attention layers while bypassing linear-recurrent states (Gated DeltaNet / Mamba-2), cutting off-task hijacking by $21\times$ ($57.9\% \to 2.7\%$) while retaining $92.2\%$ of full-text mutual information at zero extra KV allocation; and (3) a Packed DAG-GRPO Trainer, which packs all episode branches into one block-sparse attention pass and assigns counterfactual branch credit via leave-one-branch-out marginal rewards.
  Trained end-to-end with GRPO on Qwen3.5-4B over a sparse BM25 corpus, Octopus reaches $49.6\%$ accuracy on BrowseComp-Plus ($+17.5$ points over single-agent GRPO at 143K context and $+12.5$ points over text-summary multi-agent RL) and $51.2\%$ on OpenResearcher, outperforming dense-retrieval agents up to $250\times$ larger while increasing training throughput $3.3\times$ and cutting peak KV memory $2.8\times$.

</details>


### [25] [Uncertainty calibration in Bayesian M/EEG source imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06979)
*Ismail Huseynov,Mohammad Orabe,Srikantan Nagarajan,Stefan Haufe*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出基于仿真的不确定性量化与校准框架，比较BMN+sLORETA与SBL+S-FLEX两种经验贝叶斯源成像方法的后验可信区域校准性，并引入等渗后校准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: M/EEG源成像虽需可靠的不确定性估计，但经验贝叶斯逆方法的后验可信区域很少被实证校准检验；作者希望评估自适应噪声学习能否带来近校准的不确定性。

Method: 构建仿真框架，针对具闭式条件高斯后验的经验贝叶斯逆方法（BMN与SBL，后者配合S-FLEX稀疏基场展开）。推导BMN的源方差与噪声方差联合更新（证据最大化）；采用等渗回归基于经验覆盖率对可信区域水平做事后重校准；仿真覆盖固定/自由方向模型、多SNR、源稀疏度及个体头模型，噪声设定分oracle、基线估计与自适应学习三种；并应用于真实听觉MEG数据。

Result: 校准前，SBL+S-FLEX的Wasserstein定位误差更低、后验不确定性更小、覆盖率更接近名义可信度；等渗重校准使两者均改善，对偏保守的BMN+sLORETA增益更大；跨SNR、稀疏度与头模型失配的迁移依赖具体条件；各噪声设定下趋势基本一致。真实听觉MEG中SBL+S-FLEX定位更聚焦、不确定性更紧，提示对双侧听觉源识别具更高统计功效。

Conclusion: 两种方法均残留失校准，需事后重校准；SBL+S-FLEX在校准性、定位精度与不确定性紧致度上整体优于BMN+sLORETA，但重校准参数的跨条件迁移仍受限。

Abstract: M/EEG source imaging requires accurate localization and reliable uncertainty estimates, yet posterior credible regions are rarely assessed for empirical calibration. We present a simulation-based framework for uncertainty quantification and calibration of empirical Bayesian inverse methods with closed-form conditional Gaussian posterior summaries. We compare Bayesian minimum norm (BMN) with sLORETA depth weighting and sparse Bayesian learning (SBL) with sparse basis field expansions (S-FLEX). S-FLEX allows retained coefficients to contribute nonzero marginal posterior variance at locations where voxel-wise SBL yields degenerate variances. For BMN, we derive joint source- and noise-variance updates through evidence maximization. We test whether adaptive noise learning yields near-calibrated uncertainties. Both methods retain residual miscalibration, motivating post-hoc isotonic recalibration of credible-region levels using empirical coverage. Simulations assess fixed- and free-orientation models across signal-to-noise ratios (SNR), source sparsity levels, and subject-specific head models, using oracle, baseline-estimated, and adaptively learned noise. Before recalibration, SBL+S-FLEX yields lower Wasserstein localization error, smaller posterior uncertainty, and coverage closer to nominal credibility than BMN+sLORETA. Isotonic recalibration improves both methods, with larger gains for conservative BMN+sLORETA posteriors; transfer across mismatched SNR, sparsity, and head models remains condition dependent. Noise specifications yield broadly consistent reconstruction and uncertainty trends within each estimator. In real auditory MEG data, SBL+S-FLEX provides more focal localization and tighter uncertainties than BMN+sLORETA, suggesting greater statistical power for identifying bilateral auditory sources.

</details>


### [26] [Big Models, Small Gains: Frozen Forecasters as Classifiers on MONSTER](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06988)
*Rishit Jain,Rishit Kar,Chetashri Bhadane,Nilesh Patil*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文对两个预测基础模型（Reverso、Chronos-Bolt）的冻结表征进行受控基准测试，发现在MONSTER基准的6个数据集、1%/10%/100%标注比例下，预训练表征几乎无法超越强非预训练基线（17个设定中15个更差、2个持平），尽管相对随机权重版本有明显提升（最多+23个百分点）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 预测类基础模型越来越多地被当作冻结特征提取器用于时间序列分类，但其预训练表征是否真的优于无需预训练的强分类器尚不清楚；核心疑问是这些表征能否提供超越模型架构本身、尤其是在标注数据稀缺时的有用信息。

Method: 在MONSTER基准的6个大规模数据集上，用1%、10%、100%训练标签做受控基准测试；将Reverso与Chronos-Bolt的冻结表征分别与四种成熟时序分类方法、以及相同模型的随机权重版本进行对比；并做补充实验：恢复幅度信息、微调、构造预测误差特征。

Result: 预训练相对随机权重在6个数据集中的5个上提升分类精度，最高提升23个百分点；但冻结表征在任何设定下都未稳定超越最强非预训练基线：17个设定中15个更差、2个持平。恢复幅度信息可挽回部分差距，而微调与预测误差特征无法弥合差距。

Conclusion: 评估预测基础模型用于分类时，必须引入强任务特定基线与随机权重对照；预训练带来的增益主要体现为相对同架构随机权重的优势，而非超越强监督分类器的绝对优势。

Abstract: Forecasting foundation models are increasingly used as frozen feature extractors for time series classification, but it remains unclear whether their pretrained representations offer an advantage over strong classifiers that do not require pretraining. This raises an important question: do these representations provide useful information beyond what the model architecture alone can capture, particularly when labelled data are scarce? We present a controlled benchmarking of two forecasting models, Reverso and Chronos-Bolt, on six large datasets from the MONSTER benchmark, using 1%, 10%, and 100% of the available training labels. We compare their frozen representations against four established time series classification methods and random-weight versions of the same forecasting models. Our results show that pretraining improves classification accuracy over random-weight counterparts on five of the six datasets, with gains of up to 23 percentage points. However, the frozen representations do not reliably outperform the strongest non-pretrained baseline in any setting: they perform worse in 15 of 17 settings and tie in the remaining two. Further experiments show that restoring amplitude information recovers part of the performance gap, while fine-tuning and forecast-error features do not close it. These findings highlight the importance of strong task specific baselines and random weight controls when evaluating forecasting foundation models for classification. Our code is available at https://github.com/Rachet2006/frozen-forecasters-monster.

</details>


### [27] [Robust Stream Classification using Time Neutralising Decision Trees](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06991)
*Brandon Gower-Winter,Georg Krempl*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Time Neutralising Trees (TNT)——一种在概念漂移数据流下通过"中和"过期样本并按需重新纳入的决策树，在24个真实/半真实数据流上取得最优性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据流存在概念漂移，传统做法依赖漂移检测器触发重训练或简单重置（reset）。而当代研究已放宽"实例只可观察一次"的假设，并显示在可存储实例时批学习算法常优于流学习算法，因此需要一种既能利用历史样本、又能应对漂移的树模型。

Method: TNT为决策树架构：训练过程中依据流的当前上下文，将不再相关的旧训练样本过滤（"中和"）掉；若这些实例随后重新变得相关，则将其重新纳入训练。

Result: 在USP DS数据仓库的24个真实与半真实分类数据流上，与相关算法对比，TNT在多种概念漂移类型下取得best-in-class性能，证据充分。

Conclusion: 以样本"中和/再纳入"替代漂移检测+重置的范式，可实现稳健且高性能的数据流分类。

Abstract: Data Streams are characterised as a potentially infinite source of data subject to non-stationarity. This non-stationarity, also called Concept Drift, often results in deployed predictive models having to be perpetually retrained as the underlying data generating process of the stream changes over time. Drift Detectors are a class of methods used to identify these changes, triggering these retraining procedures. Historically, Stream Learning has operated under the assumption that instances are only seen once, and cannot be stored for later use. Contemporary work relaxes this assumption, yet drift detection and simple resetting procedures are still ubiquitous.
  Building on related work which has shown that traditional Batch Learning algorithms often outperform Stream Learning algorithms when instances can be stored, we introduce Time Neutralising Trees (TNT), a Decision Tree architecture that enables robust stream classification in settings with Concept Drift. During training, TNT will filter out ("neutralise") old training samples that are no longer relevant given the current context of the stream. If those instances become relevant again, TNT will then re-include them. We evaluate TNT, and related algorithms on 24 real and semi-real classification data streams from the USP DS repository. Results show strong evidence that TNT achieves best-in-class performance on a wide-range of data streams with varying concept drift types.

</details>


### [28] [LowRAM: Low-Rank Keys and Amortised Values for Composable KV Cache Compression at Test-Time](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07025)
*Teresa Delgado,Hugo Hazard,Martin Benfeghoul,Jun Wang,Zafeirios Fountas*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出LowRAM：利用"同一注意力头内value可由key预测"这一互补冗余轴做value缓存摊销，并配合跨层低秩key压缩，形成非对称KV缓存压缩流水线，4×近无损，组合量化与驱逐可达26.9×压缩并保留94.5%性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归解码依赖KV cache，其显存随上下文长度线性增长，限制了batch size与吞吐。现有后训练压缩方法主要利用token、层、特征维度冗余或降低数值精度，尚未显式利用key与value之间的结构性差异作为新的压缩轴。

Method: 1) Value cache amortisation：观察到每个注意力头内value可由对应key高度预测，测试时为每个头拟合轻量预测器，仅对重建误差最大的token存储残差，实现4×近无损value压缩；预测器可经元学习初始化以降低prefill开销，或在允许一次性成本的场景（如multi-query inference）下从头拟合。2) Key压缩：跨层低秩key压缩。3) 二者组成非对称压缩流水线LowRAM，解码时仅重建所选子集的KV对以控制开销。

Result: 在三个模型族上保持近无损；4×压缩下峰值解码吞吐提升至5×；与量化、驱逐组合后在长上下文上达26.9×压缩，保留基线94.5%性能。

Conclusion: 将key与value视为结构上不同的对象，可实现高效、激进且可组合的KV cache压缩，开辟了与现有token/层/维度冗余及精度压缩正交的新方向。

Abstract: Autoregressive decoding relies on the KV cache, whose memory footprint grows linearly with context length and caps batch size and throughput. Existing post-training methods exploit redundancy across tokens, layers, and feature dimensions, or lower numerical precision. We identify a complementary axis: within each attention head, values are largely predictable from their corresponding keys. We exploit this with value cache amortisation, which fits a lightweight per-head predictor at test time and stores residuals only for the tokens it reconstructs worst, giving $4\times$ near-lossless value compression. The predictor can be initialised by meta-learning for substantially reduced prefill overhead, or fitted from scratch in settings that allow a one-off cost, such as multi-query inference. We pair value amortisation with cross-layer low-rank key compression to produce LowRAM, an asymmetric compression pipeline that stays close to lossless across three model families. By reconstructing only a selected subset of KV pairs at each decoding step, LowRAM can keep decoding overhead minimal and achieve up to $5\times$ higher peak decode throughput at $4\times$ compression. LowRAM further composes with quantisation and eviction, reaching $26.9\times$ compression at $94.5\%$ of baseline performance on long contexts. These results show that treating keys and values as structurally distinct enables efficient, aggressive, and composable KV cache compression.

</details>


### [29] [Stochastic Dynamics of Generative AI: From Autoregressive Models to Interacting LLM Networks and Neural SDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07065)
*Vikram Krishnamurthy,Yiming Zhang,Luke Snow*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从随机动力系统视角统一刻画三类生成式AI模型：将LLM表述为高维非线性自回归模型，用平均场ODE刻画多LLM交互网络的集体行为，并以神经SDE作为路径空间的连续时间生成模型，同时引入基于粗糙路径分析的Wiener空间cubature方法做高效训练。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有生成式AI模型（LLM、多智能体LLM网络、连续时间生成模型）缺乏统一的概率与动力学刻画，尤其对交互网络中的涌现行为与不规则采样轨迹的生成缺乏理论工具。

Method: (1) 将LLM建模为带注意力依赖的高维非线性自回归模型，把条件极大似然预训练与序列token生成统一到概率框架；(2) 用平均场ODE描述互作LLM网络的集体动力学，分析优先连接与结构性偏差（含类型相关的玻璃天花板效应）；(3) 用神经SDE建模连续时间随机轨迹，并以基于粗糙路径分析的Wiener空间cubature方法替代蒙特卡洛估计进行训练。

Result: 建立了三类模型各自的概率/动力学表述：LLM的条件MLE—序列生成的对应关系；网络中涌现的优先连接与持续性结构偏差（含类型依赖的玻璃天花板效应）；cubature方法作为比蒙特卡洛更高效的训练估计手段。

Conclusion: 三个设定共同覆盖概率预测、交互网络中的信息交换与涌现行为、以及路径空间生成建模，构成一个随机动力系统视角下的生成式AI统一分析框架。

Abstract: This paper develops a stochastic dynamical-systems perspective on three classes of generative AI models. First, we formulate large language models (LLMs) as high-dimensional nonlinear autoregressive models with attention-based dependencies. This formulation connects pretraining by conditional maximum likelihood to sequential token generation and provides a probabilistic interpretation of LLM operation. Second, we consider networks of interacting LLMs and characterize their collective behavior through mean-field ordinary differential equations. These dynamics provide a framework for understanding emergent preferential attachment and persistent structural biases, including type-dependent glass-ceiling effects. Third, we consider neural stochastic differential equations (SDEs) as continuous-time generative models for stochastic trajectories, including those observed at irregular time intervals. We discuss their training with particular emphasis on Wiener-space cubature methods based on rough-path analysis as an efficient alternative to Monte Carlo estimation. Together, the three settings address probabilistic prediction, information exchange and emergent behavior in interacting networks, and path-space generative modeling.

</details>


### [30] [Finite-Time Convergence and Regret Guarantees for Neural Reinforcement Learning in POMDPs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07069)
*Jingjuan Huang,Zhiyao Zhang,Alvaro Velasquez,Nathaniel D. Bastian,Jia Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程（POMDP）中基于非线性神经网络值函数近似的强化学习，首次给出有限记忆POMDP下有限时间次线性悔界的理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: POMDP中长程历史依赖导致计算困难；已有研究表明满足滤波稳定性假设的POMDP可用有限记忆窗口内的截断历史构造"超状态"MDP近似，其价值误差随窗口长度衰减。但现有有限时间收敛分析要么局限于过简化的线性函数近似，要么依赖被POMDP轨迹违背的i.i.d.采样oracle，限制了理论结果的实用性。

Method: i) 建立新的有限状态MDP逐点近似误差界，为算法设计与分析奠定基础；ii) 将时序差分（TD）学习应用于POMDP生成的超状态转移，以超状态MDP的Bellman不动点为目标，证明非线性神经值函数近似下的有限时间critic误差界；iii) 将神经critic近似误差界与策略镜像下降框架结合。

Result: 得到相对最优POMDP价值的有限时间次线性悔界；数值实验验证了理论结果。据作者所知，这是文献中首个针对有限记忆POMDP、基于神经函数近似的有限时间次线性悔界。

Conclusion: 通过在超状态MDP代理上结合神经TD critic与策略镜像下降，可在不依赖i.i.d.采样假设、且采用非线性神经网络近似的条件下，为有限记忆POMDP提供有限时间次线性悔界的理论保证。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) in Partially Observable Markov Decision Processes (POMDPs) is computationally challenging due to the curse of long-range dependence on history. Fortunately, recent studies have shown that a range of POMDPs that satisfy the filter stability assumption can be well approximated by a superstate Markov Decision Process (MDP) based on a truncated history in a finite-memory window, with a value gap that decays with the window length. However, existing finite-time convergence analyses on such superstate MDP surrogates are either limited to over-simplified linear function approximation or rely on an i.i.d. sampling oracle that is violated by POMDP trajectories, both of which severely limit the applicability of these theoretical results in practice. This motivates us to bridge the gap by investigating RL in POMDPs with nonlinear neural value function approximation. Our main contributions are threefold: i) We first establish a new finite-state MDP point-wise approximation error bound, which lays the foundation for our RL algorithm design and analysis in POMDPs; ii) By applying Temporal-Difference (TD) learning to POMDP-generated superstate transitions and targeting the Bellman fixed point of the superstate MDP, we prove a finite-time critic error bound with nonlinear neural value function approximation; and iii) By combining the neural critic function approximation error bound with the policy mirror descent framework, we obtain a finite-time sublinear regret against the optimal POMDP value. To the best of our knowledge, such a finite-time sublinear regret based on neural function approximation is the first in the literature for finite-memory POMDPs. Our numerical experiments also verify our theoretical results.

</details>


### [31] [Shaping & Monitoring Models through their Internal Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07071)
*Thomas Fel,Matthew Kowal,Matt Feiszli,Satchel Grant,Andrew Lee,Chris Earls,Fatih Dinc,Vasudev Shyam,Usha Bhalla,Leon Bergen,Siddharth Boppana,Ching Fang,Michael Byun,Tristan Gallent,Nicholas Wang,Tal Haklay,Dron Hazra,Neehar Kondapaneni,Connor Watts,Ryan Panwar,Thomas Dooms,Jack Merullo,Atticus Geiger,Ekdeep Singh Lubana,Thomas McGrath,Owen Lewis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 Mechanistic Shaping 统一框架，通过内部表征塑造模型行为，并在多领域/多规模/多范式下验证其有效性，同时给出监测器层级与三种规避几何机制来分析"被塑造概念是否仍可监测"。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有训练方法（如 RLHF、DPO 等）只能约束模型输出层面"表达什么概念"，难以约束模型"依据什么概念做决策"（如简历筛选不依赖性别）。作者希望用可解释性（内部表征）直接作为训练信号，统一地约束模型所表达与所依赖的概念。

Method: 提出 Mechanistic Shaping：在训练过程中对内部表征施加约束，使模型既不在输出/表征中表达违规概念，也不依赖敏感概念做决策。在视觉、医学影像、语言等域，最高 32B 参数规模，以及分类、SFT、DPO、GRPO 等范式上验证。针对"训练是否破坏可监测性"，引入监测器类别层级，并刻画三种规避几何机制（概念漂移离开监测器、在层中被擦除、以高阶统计中的非线性编码存留，后者用乘积码构造解释）。

Result: Mechanistic Shaping 显著降低目标行为，同时保持任务性能。被塑造概念大多仍可监测：重拟合的线性探针在多数设置下能恢复概念；当梯度不流经原探针时，原探针依然可靠。三种机制可被对应的监测器检测到。

Conclusion: 可解释性可作为训练工具，用于约束模型所表达与所依赖的概念；但其可靠性依赖于监测器层级的严谨设计，因此需要建立"基于可解释性的训练"这一系统性科学。

Abstract: We propose Mechanistic Shaping, a unified framework for shaping models through their internal representations. Mechanistic Shaping is applied during training so that models follow specifications (rules) about which concepts they express, measured via their outputs or internal representations (e.g., no harmful content), and also which concepts they rely on, i.e., what they use to make their decisions (e.g., not a candidate's gender when screening a résumé). Across multiple domains (vision, medical imaging, language), scales (up to 32B parameters), and training paradigms (classification, SFT, DPO, GRPO), we find that Mechanistic Shaping substantially reduces targeted behaviors while preserving task performance. Since training against a representation can also modify it, we ask whether shaped concepts remain monitorable, a question that depends on the class of monitors considered. We therefore introduce a hierarchy of such classes, which separates a concept that escapes one probe from a concept that is truly gone. We characterize three geometric mechanisms of circumvention: the concept can drift away from the monitor, be erased from the layer, or persist in higher-order statistics as a nonlinear encoding, which we explain through a product-code construction. Although other mechanisms may arise in different settings or at larger scales, these three account for all of our observations, and each can be detected by a matching monitor. In practice, we find that shaped concepts largely remain monitorable: refitted linear probes recover the concept in most settings, and the original probe remains reliable whenever gradients do not flow through it. Taken together, these results point to the potential of using interpretability as a tool for training, and call for a correspondingly rigorous science of interpretability-based training.

</details>


### [32] [Optimistic Hedge in General-Sum Games: Fundamental Limits and Regret Bounds via Temporal Approximation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07078)
*Yuval Dagan,Nadav Eisen,Liad Erez,Tomer Koren*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究Optimistic Hedge（带一步外推的乘性权重更新）的 regrets 界。证明答案取决于玩家数量：两人博弈中存在学习率使 regret 为 O(n log n) 且与时间跨度无关；三人及以上博弈则无法做到，regret 可达 2^{Ω(mn)}，学习率较大时甚至线性增长。上界基于时间局部化近似框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Optimistic Hedge 是求解博弈的自然 regret 最小化动力学，但其 regret 刻画长期是开放问题。已有工作：Daskalakis 等证明固定学习率下 regret 为 polylog(T)；Ha 证明可选学习率使 regret 与 T 无关，但该界非构造性且对动作数 n 的依赖不显式，因此遗留问题：Optimistic Hedge 能否达到多项式于博弈规模、与 T 无关的常数 regret 界。

Method: 上界：提出针对 Optimistic Hedge 的时间近似（temporal approximation）框架。从 Daskalakis 等的基于方差的 RVU 界出发，将时间局部窗口内的损失近似为行为良好的函数，使其总变差仅占累积方差的很小比例。下界：构造对抗性博弈随玩家数与动作数增长。

Result: 两人博弈：存在学习率使 Optimistic Hedge 的 regret 为 O(n log n) 且与 T 无关。三人博弈：对任何至多常数级的学习率及足够大的 T，存在 n 动作博弈使某玩家 regret 为 2^{Ω(n)}；一般对 m≥3，Regret 为 2^{Ω(mn)}；当学习率高于 2^{-Ω(mn)} 时该 regret 甚至随 T 线性增长。此外，该框架还给出 Ha 的 O(√m log n log T) 界的显著简化证明。

Conclusion: 多项式于博弈规模、与时间跨度无关的常数 regret 是否可行取决于玩家数：两人博弈可行（O(n log n)），三人及以上不可行（指数级下界，且大学习率下线性增长）。时间近似框架同时统一并简化了已有的 polylog 与 √m 界，为分析 Optimistic Hedge 提供了有力工具。

Abstract: Optimistic Hedge, a multiplicative weight update with one step of extrapolation, is one of the most natural regret-minimizing dynamics for games, and characterizing its regret has been a longstanding open question. Daskalakis, Fishelson and Golowich proved that if all $m$ players in a finite general-sum game follow Optimistic Hedge with a fixed learning rate, each player's regret is polylogarithmic in the horizon $T$. Subsequently, Ha showed that an appropriate choice of learning rate makes each player's regret bounded independently of $T$. This bound, however, is non-constructive and its dependence on the number of actions $n$ is not explicit, leaving open whether Optimistic Hedge can achieve a constant regret bound which is polynomial in the size of the game.
  We show that the answer hinges on the number of players. In two-player games, there is a learning rate for which Optimistic Hedge guarantees $O(n\log n)$ regret independent of the horizon. In three-player games, however, no such bound is possible: for any learning rate up to an absolute constant and for sufficiently large horizon $T$, there is a game with $n$ actions per player in which some player's regret is $2^{Ω(n)}$, and more generally, for every $m\ge3$ we construct $m$-player games in which some player's regret is $2^{Ω(mn)}$; for learning rates above $2^{-Ω(mn)}$, that player's regret even grows linearly in $T$.
  Our upper bound is based on a temporal approximation framework for the analysis of Optimistic Hedge. Starting from the variance-based RVU bound of Daskalakis et al., we approximate the losses over time-localized windows by well-behaved functions, whose total variation is a small fraction of their cumulative variance. In $m$-player games, the same framework gives a significantly simplified proof of the recent $O(\sqrt{m}\log n\log T)$ regret bound due to Ha.

</details>


### [33] [Server-Side Sparse Assignment for Federated Mixture-of-Experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07093)
*Xue Zheng,Aylin Yener*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了服务端稀疏专家分配框架 S³-MoE，在部分观测下估计客户端-专家相关性并通过利用-探索机制精炼分配，同时给出相关性估计误差与精确 Top-K 恢复的理论保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘设备异构（数据分布多样、内存与算力受限），现有联邦 MoE 的部分专家方法依赖启发式全专家池观测或追求系统级负载均衡，无法保证从部分观测中恢复对特定客户端真正相关的专家。

Method: 服务端稀疏专家分配框架：由部分观测估计客户端特定的专家相关性，再通过 exploit–explore 机制迭代精炼分配；并建立相关性估计误差界与精确 Top-K 恢复保证。

Result: 在 CIFAR-100 上用 CNN-based MoE 验证：S³-MoE 能恢复任务相关的客户端-专家偏好并减少因客户端间相关专家选择导致的冲突；相比 Full-20 FedAvg（每客户端可用全部 20 个专家做本地 Top-5 路由），仅加载 5 个专家即获得高 1.31 个百分点的平均最终准确率。

Conclusion: 在部分观测下以服务端集中式分配替代启发式策略，可在显著降低每客户端专家加载量的同时提升精度，并具备可证明的专家恢复保障。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly recognized for their remarkable capabilities; federated learning (FL) offers a promising framework for collaboratively fine-tuning LLMs on edge devices while achieving data privacy. However, edge devices are inherently heterogeneous: clients may exhibit diverse data distributions and resource constraints, including limited memory and computation budgets. Recent federated Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) approaches address these challenges by using sparse expert activation or partial expert loading. However, existing partial-expert methods rely on heuristic full-pool observations or prioritize system-wide load balance, without guarantees for recovering client-specific relevant experts from partial observations. We propose $S^3$-MoE, a server-side sparse expert assignment framework that estimates client-specific expert relevance from partial observations and refines assignments through an exploit--explore mechanism. We also establish relevance-estimation error and exact Top-$K$ recovery guarantees. Experiments using a CNN-based MoE on CIFAR-100 show that $S^3$-MoE recovers task-dependent client--expert preferences and reduces conflicts caused by correlated expert selection across clients. Compared with Full-20 FedAvg, which gives each client access to all 20 experts for local Top-5 routing, $S^3$-MoE achieves 1.31 percentage points higher mean final accuracy while loading only $5$ experts per client.

</details>


### [34] [A Night at the LLM Zoo: Data Conditioned Flow Matching for Generating Pretrained LLM Weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.07103)
*Scott Biggs,Bedionita Soro,Saumya Gupta,Bruno Andreis,Sung Ju Hwang*

Main category: cs.LG
