Abstract: Real-time voice agents need a speech recognizer that never waits for future audio and whose memory per call does not grow with the length of the call. We present E-Gaborformer, a strictly causal encoder that combines Gated DeltaNet-2 recurrent layers with sliding-window attention in a 3:1 ratio, and builds every temporal convolution from a fixed dictionary of causal Gabor atoms. Its streaming state is constant, about 19 MB per call at production scale including the prediction network, where a causal-attention encoder of the same size accumulates over 1 GB after ten minutes. In a controlled causal study on LibriSpeech, E-Gaborformer leaves the initial plateau earlier, reaches a given accuracy up to 18% sooner, and matches or beats an E-Branchformer trained with the identical front end and recipe. At production scale, a 0.6B-parameter Hebrew and English streaming transducer built on E-Gaborformer has 28 to 31% lower word error rate (WER) than NVIDIA's Nemotron 3.5 ASR Streaming, a model of the same size, on English voice-agent traffic, and up to 20% lower Hebrew WER than our previous production system. The same weights serve strictly causal and 480 ms-lookahead decoding with no loss in accuracy, and a two-class formulation of the RNN-T lattice trains the transducer 2.6x faster with exact loss and gradients.

</details>


### [138] [Scaling Neural Motor Decoding via Decoupled Behavioral Pretraining](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08263)
*Divyansha Lachi,Vinam Arora,Shivashriganesh P. Mahato,Alexandre Andre,Eva L. Dyer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出BEEMO框架，解耦行为与神经表征学习，可用成对神经-行为记录与非配对行为数据的任意混合进行训练，从而以低成本的行为数据扩展神经解码性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有神经解码依赖大规模成对神经-行为记录，采集昂贵、具侵入性且难以扩展；而行为数据丰富易得。作者据此提问：能否解耦行为与神经表征学习，使解码受益于易得的行为数据。

Method: BEEMO为灵活的多session框架：行为解码器直接从行为轨迹学习运动动力学；当存在神经数据时，通过cross-attention层注入神经活动，使行为监督与神经监督可独立扩展。支持任意比例的配对与非配对数据混合训练，并可仅用行为预训练。

Result: 在多项非人灵长类皮层内运动数据集与任务上，加入非配对行为数据提升了解码性能，尤其在新任务迁移时更明显；加入少量目标任务行为数据可进一步改善迁移。仅行为预训练（无神经预训练）即达到与在更大神经数据集上预训练模型相当的性能。

Conclusion: 解耦神经与行为数据开辟了新的扩展维度：更廉价易采的非配对行为数据可直接用于提升解码；扩展行为数据是迈向跨被试、跨任务泛化神经解码模型的实用且高性价比路径。

Abstract: Recent neural decoding approaches achieve strong performance by training on large collections of paired neural-behavioral recordings, but such datasets are expensive, invasive, and difficult to scale. In contrast, behavioral data is abundant and easy to collect, raising the question of whether neural decoding can benefit from decoupling behavioral and neural representation learning. We introduce BEEMO, a flexible multi-session framework that enables training with arbitrary mixtures of paired neural-behavioral recordings and unpaired behavioral data. A behavior decoder learns movement dynamics directly from behavioral trajectories, while neural activity is incorporated through cross-attention layers when available, allowing behavioral and neural supervision to scale independently. Across multiple intracortical motor datasets and tasks in nonhuman primates, we show that incorporating unpaired behavioral data improves decoding performance, particularly when transferring to new tasks, and that adding even a small amount of behavioral data from the target task further improves transfer. By decoupling neural and behavioral data, we unlock a new dimension for scaling: unpaired behavioral data, which is substantially cheaper and easier to collect than paired neural recordings, can be used directly to improve decoding performance. We further show that behavior-only pretraining, without any neural pretraining, achieves performance comparable to models pretrained on substantially larger neural datasets. Together, our results suggest that scaling behavioral data offers a practical and cost-effective path toward neural decoding models that generalize across subjects and tasks. Code is available at https://github.com/nerdslab/beemo.

</details>


### [139] [Accelerating Safe Reinforcement Learning with Massive Parallelism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08273)
*Joonyoung Lim,Younghwan Yoo*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 针对大规模并行（短rollout、多环境）训练与CMDP全episode成本约束之间的不匹配，提出将交错环境重置视为短轨迹段的相位混合分布，并据此构建相位聚合成本估计器，结合约束收紧框架实现可扩展的on-policy安全RL方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模并行训练提升了数据吞吐与wall-clock效率，但其短rollout设定与CMDP在完整episode成本回报上定义的安全约束相冲突，直接套用会导致约束估计偏差。

Method: 将staggered environment resets建模为短轨迹段上的phase-mixture分布，分析相位覆盖引入的偏差；据此提出phase-aggregated cost estimator，从短rollout批次重构episode级成本估计；并引入有限样本不确定性与分布失配，纳入constraint-tightening框架。

Result: 在基于MJX实现的Safety Gym上，所提方法性能与非大规模并行实现相当，同时显著缩短wall-clock训练时间。

Conclusion: 相位混合分析与相位聚合成本估计可有效弥合大规模并行训练与CMDP约束定义之间的差距，使安全RL在保持性能的前提下获得显著的计算效率收益。

Abstract: We investigate the problem of scaling safe reinforcement learning (safe RL) to massively parallel training regimes, where a large number of environments are exe- cuted with short rollout horizons. Although this setting improves data throughput and wall-clock efficiency, it creates a mismatch with constrained Markov decision processes (CMDPs), whose safety constraints are defined over full-episode cost returns. We address this mismatch by analyzing staggered environment resets as a phase-mixture distribution over short trajectory segments. This analysis charac- terizes the bias induced by phase coverage and motivates a phase-aggregated cost estimator that reconstructs episode-level cost estimates from short rollout batches. We further incorporate finite-sample uncertainty and distribution mismatch into a constraint-tightening framework, yielding a scalable Safe RL method compatible with on-policy optimization. Using an MJX-based implementation of Safety Gym, our experiments show that the proposed method achieves performance comparable to non-massively parallel implementations while substantially reducing wall-clock training time.

</details>


### [140] [PersonaAgent: Retrieval-Augmented Transaction Anomaly Scoring with Refreshable Multi-Entity Persona Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08094)
*Zhengyu Fang,Liang Wang,Yingtong Dou,Yuzhong Chen,Shubham Jain,Junpeng Wang,Xiran Fan,Menghai Pan,Jiarui Sun,Yan Zheng,Yiwei Cai*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: PersonaAgent是一种代理式交易异常检测框架，将实体的最新行为信号编码为"persona"并存入可刷新记忆，推理时由轻量适配器检索并增强基础异常分类器的评分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模支付网络中实体（卡、商户等）及其交互对异常检测至关重要，但实体间交易模式的变化频率通常高于生产模型刷新周期；同时实体嵌入表面临高基数和稀疏交互问题。

Method: 以可刷新记忆存储实体最新行为信号（persona）；离线通过每周调度的批处理任务提取最新行为信号并更新记忆；推理时用轻量适配器从记忆中检索每笔交易对应实体的行为信号，用于增强基础异常分类器的打分。

Result: 在Visa与多种异常检测模型的综合基准测试中，PersonaAgent可高效打分（CPU上1000笔交易批次耗时1-2 ms），PR-AUC相对提升0.65-6.58%，部署中生产业务指标相对提升8.93-14.24%。

Conclusion: 通过将实体行为的解耦表征与线上分类器分离并支持周期性刷新，PersonaAgent在保持低延迟的同时有效缓解模式漂移与嵌入稀疏/高基数问题，提升检测效果与业务指标。

Abstract: In large-scale payment networks, entities (card, merchant, etc.) and their interactions play a key role in transaction anomaly detection. However, transaction patterns between different entities usually change more frequently than the production model refresh schedule. Meanwhile, entity embedding tables suffer from high-cardinality and sparse interaction issues. To resolve the above challenges, we present PersonaAgent, an agentic transaction anomaly detection framework which represents the latest entity behavior signals as personas and saves them in a refreshable memory. Offline, a weekly scheduled batch job is used to extract the latest behavior signals and update them in the memory. At inference time, a lightweight adapter retrieves the corresponding entity behavior signals of each transaction from memory and uses them to augment the base anomaly classifier's scoring. Through comprehensive benchmarking with different anomaly detection models at Visa, PersonaAgent is able to score transactions efficiently (a 1,000-transaction CPU batch in 1-2 ms), lifting the PR-AUC by 0.65-6.58% and the production business metric by 8.93-14.24% relatively in deployment.

</details>


### [141] [AutoCompress: Automated Hardware-Guided Model Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08290)
*Sheldon Liang,Ali Taghibakhshi,Saurav Muralidharan,Sharath Turuvekere Sreenivas,Ashwath Aithal,Pavlo Molchanov,Nima Tajbakhsh*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 AutoCompress，一种以硬件约束（延迟、吞吐、显存）为直接目标的差异化架构搜索剪枝框架，联合优化全局与逐层结构以生成异构架构，并在多种实际部署场景下相较 Minitron 提升精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 LLM 剪枝流程多面向参数量，需人工挑选满足延迟、吞吐与 GPU 显存限制的模型尺寸；但参数量无法预测这些指标，因为它们取决于模型架构、工作负载、硬件与推理引擎，导致剪枝与实际部署约束脱节。

Method: 将硬件与工作负载约束直接嵌入可微架构搜索：联合优化全局结构与逐层结构，生成异构架构；并以经过端到端延迟、吞吐和显存实测校验的 serving-engine 成本模型作为搜索引导信号。

Result: 在长上下文本地编码、批处理数据中心服务、延迟敏感的游戏中交互三类场景评测；八个基准上的平均精度较 SOTA 的 Minitron 分别提升 6.57、2.35、0.57 个百分点（RTX 5070、RTX 5080、H100）；并将 Nemotron 3.5 Super 由 120B 压缩至 74B，在单张 B200 BF16、128K 上下文服务下保留 95% 的平均基准分数。

Conclusion: 以实测校验的成本模型引导的硬件感知异构剪枝，可在满足延迟、吞吐与显存约束的同时优于面向参数量的剪枝方法，验证了将部署约束纳入架构搜索的有效性与跨硬件、跨场景的泛化性。

Abstract: LLM pruning must preserve quality while meeting hardware- and workload-specific constraints on latency, throughput, and memory. Existing pruning workflows often target parameter counts, requiring practitioners to manually select model sizes that meet latency, throughput, and GPU memory limits. However, parameter count alone does not predict these metrics, which depend on the model architecture, workload, hardware, and serving engine. We introduce AutoCompress, a hardware-guided compression framework that directly incorporates such constraints into differentiable architecture search. AutoCompress jointly optimizes global and per-layer structure to produce heterogeneous architectures, guided by serving-engine cost models validated against end-to-end latency, throughput, and memory measurements. We evaluate AutoCompress on long-context local coding, batched datacenter serving, and latency-sensitive in-game interaction. Compared to the state-of-the-art Minitron pruning framework, AutoCompress improves average accuracy across eight benchmarks by 6.57, 2.35, and 0.57 percentage points on RTX 5070, RTX 5080, and H100, respectively. AutoCompress also compresses Nemotron 3.5 Super from 120B to 74B parameters for single-B200 BF16 serving at 128K context, retaining 95% of its mean benchmark score.

</details>


### [142] [How Far Can Untrained Neural Networks Go in Anomaly Detection? From Tables to Pixels](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08136)
*Hyojun Yi,Geunyoung Kim,Hogeon Seo,Seunghyoung Ryu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出PRISM，用"固定随机非线性特征 + 正常参考马氏距离打分"实现无训练、无预训练的异常检测，在表格与视觉任务上取得有竞争力的结果，并作为"学习前基线"评估学习特征的增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 异常检测通常被框定为表示学习问题，默认需要可学习特征或AD专用优化。该视角可能忽视异常与正常参考本身的内在可分性，因此作者追问：在完全不训练网络的情况下，异常检测能走多远。

Method: 提出PRISM框架：将固定随机非线性特征与基于正常参考的Mahalanobis评分结合，分为表格检测版本PRISM-T与稠密视觉定位版本PRISM-V；不进行神经权重优化，也不使用预训练表示。并给出理论分析，刻画非线性变换如何影响Mahalanobis得分差距中的均值项与协方差项，辅以实证分析检验得分可分性。

Result: 在ADBench的47个表格数据集上，PRISM-T平均AUROC达86.4%（最高），平均AUPRC达65.9%（第二高）；在MVTec AD与VisA共27个视觉类别上，PRISM-V的图像级与像素级平均AUROC分别为90.4%和96.4%。

Conclusion: PRISM既是有竞争力的异常检测器，也是"学习前基线"，可用于衡量学习表示在异常检测中的实际增益；结果表明相当一部分性能来自异常的内在可分性而非特征学习。

Abstract: Anomaly detection (AD) is often framed as a representation-learning problem, where strong performance is expected to require learned features or AD-specific optimization. However, this view can overlook the intrinsic separability of anomalies from normal references, even in the absence of learned representations. We therefore investigate how far untrained neural networks can go in anomaly detection. To this end, we propose PRISM, a framework combining fixed random nonlinear features with normal-reference Mahalanobis scoring for tabular detection and dense visual localization. Across 47 tabular datasets in ADBench, PRISM-T achieves the highest mean AUROC (86.4%) and the second-highest mean AUPRC (65.9%) among the compared methods. On MVTec AD and VisA, covering 27 visual categories, PRISM-V achieves mean image-level and pixel-level AUROCs of 90.4% and 96.4%, respectively. Notably, both models achieve these results without neural weight optimization or pretrained representations. A theoretical analysis further characterizes how nonlinear transformations affect the mean and covariance contributions to the Mahalanobis score gap, while empirical analyses examine the resulting score separability. These findings establish PRISM as both a competitive anomaly detector and a prior-to-learning baseline for assessing the added value of learned representations in anomaly detection.

</details>


### [143] [Hybrid Machine Learning for Imbalanced Landing-Phase Safety Event Prediction from Flight Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08386)
*Taha Asghar,Bilal Mufti,Syed Qasim Zaheer,Luqman Ahmed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出融合LSTM自编码器重构误差与统计飞行历史特征的可解释混合框架，经类别加权XGBoost实现着陆阶段多类安全事件预测，跨三个机场平均宏召回率>0.90。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 着陆阶段安全事件预测需在强不平衡多元飞行数据中检测稀有异常进近，同时需刻画耦合时序行为并区分不同机场运行情境下的事件特异特征，这是核心建模难点。

Method: 在正常飞行窗口上训练LSTM自编码器，以其连续重构误差作为偏离常规进近的异常描述子；将该描述子与参数级统计飞行历史特征融合，输入类别加权XGBoost分类器，区分Normal、Speed High、Path High、Flaps Late四类。基于NASA DASHlink三个到达机场的53,750个进近窗口，比较合并与分机场学习策略、组件级基准、重复流水线重训练及特征归因分析。

Result: 重复划分与重训练下各机场平均宏召回率均高于0.90，类平衡检测性能良好；组件对比证实类别加权的必要性，连续特征融合优于仅重构误差与硬门控方案；较大数据量的两个机场受益于本地专门化，最小数据量机场受益于合并训练；特征归因显示预测与垂直路径、构型、速度及进近情境等运行相关变量关联。

Conclusion: 该框架为稀有事件检测提供了可解释方案，并为依据机场级数据可得性调整飞行数据监控模型提供实践指导。

Abstract: Landing-phase safety-event prediction requires the detection of rare abnormal approaches within strongly imbalanced multivariate flight data. Capturing coupled temporal behavior while distinguishing event-specific signatures across airport operating contexts remains a central modeling challenge. This study proposes an interpretable hybrid feature-fusion framework for multiclass landing-phase safety-event prediction. A long short-term memory (LSTM) autoencoder trained on nominal flight windows characterizes deviations from routine approach behavior. Its continuous reconstruction-error descriptor is fused with parameter-specific statistical flight-history features and supplied to a class-weighted XGBoost classifier to distinguish Normal, Speed High, Path High, and Flaps Late windows. The framework is evaluated using 53,750 NASA DASHlink approach windows from three arrival airports. Pooled and airport-specific learning strategies are compared alongside component-level benchmarks, repeated pipeline retraining, and feature-attribution analysis. The results demonstrate strong class-balanced detection, with mean macro recall above 0.90 across all airports under repeated partitioning and retraining. Component comparisons establish the importance of class weighting and show that continuous feature fusion outperforms reconstruction-error-only and hard-gate alternatives. Airport-level comparisons reveal benefits from local specialization at the two airports with larger datasets and from pooled training at the airport with the smallest dataset. Feature attributions associate predictions with operationally relevant vertical-path, configuration, speed, and approach-context variables. The framework provides an interpretable approach to rare-event detection and practical guidance for adapting flight-data monitoring models to airport-level data availability.

</details>


### [144] [Detecting Weather Fronts from Satellite Observations: What Does Reanalysis Add?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08148)
*Ioannis Dravilas,Miruna Stoicescu,Roope Tervo,Carlos Horn,Oriol Hinojo Comellas,Sina Montazeri,Andrea Meraner,Ana Lucic*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该研究用同一ML模型比较仅用静止卫星红外观测与加入28个ERA5再分析场的两种配置，进行北大西洋与欧洲逐像素锋面检测。仅卫星模型CSI@150为0.402，处于不同气象部门分析间的一致范围内；加入ERA5后升至0.575（提升0.173）。低层风最难由卫星表征（R²=0.23，温度0.88），移除风或水汽对检测性能影响最大。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 锋面是中纬度降水和强天气的重要系统，需评估机器学习锋面检测方法，并量化再分析资料对检测性能的贡献，厘清卫星观测与再分析场各自的相对价值。

Method: 对同一ML模型设置两种配置，在北大西洋与欧洲区域做逐像素锋面分类：（1）仅输入静止卫星红外观测；（2）额外输入28个ERA5再分析场。采用150 km容差邻域临界成功指数（CSI@150）评估，并进行可预测性（R²）与场剔除消融分析。

Result: 仅卫星模型CSI@150=0.402，落在不同气象部门分析间的一致范围内；卫星+ERA5模型CSI@150=0.575，提升0.173。低层风最难由卫星表征（R²=0.23，温度R²=0.88）；移除风或水汽字段导致检测性能降幅最大。

Conclusion: 再分析场显著提升ML锋面检测性能，其中风与水汽信息贡献最关键；仅卫星方案已具与人工分析相当量级的能力，而融合再分析可进一步接近业务可用水平。

Abstract: Weather fronts are transition regions between air masses with contrasting thermodynamic properties and are associated with mid-latitude precipitation and severe weather. We investigate machine learning (ML) methods for detecting weather fronts and quantify the contribution of reanalysis data to performance. We compare two configurations of the same ML model for pixel-wise frontal classification over the North Atlantic and Europe: (1) using only geostationary infrared satellite observations, and (2) additionally using 28 ERA5 reanalysis fields. The satellite-only model achieves a neighbourhood critical success index at a 150 km tolerance (CSI@150) of $0.402$, which lies within the range of agreement between analyses from different meteorological services. The satellite$+$ERA5 model achieves $0.575$, an improvement of $0.173$. Our analysis shows that low-level winds are the least predictable from the satellite representation ($R^2 = 0.23$, compared with $0.88$ for temperature), while removing winds or moisture causes the largest decreases in detection performance.

</details>


### [145] [Towards interpreting any residual stream direction by scaling activation inversion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08465)
*Celeste De Schamphelaere,Juan J. Vazquez,Ari Spiesberger,Tomáš Gavenčiak,Gavin Leech,Euan Ong,Jiahai Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 MAEM（最大化激活示例元模型），用无标签 RL 目标把主体模型残差流方向映射为能强激活该方向的文本示例，效果优于语料检索与自然语言自编码器，且可由前向传播验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用文本解释残差流方向时，靠扫描语料找强激活文本受限于语料本身的内容覆盖，无法灵活扩展。

Method: 训练语言模型作为元模型，输入主体模型的残差流方向，输出示例文本；以生成文本激活与给定方向的余弦相似度为目标，构成无标签 RL 目标，从而可在多样化的方向来源上规模化训练。

Result: Qwen3.6-27B MAEM 在反演留出激活上优于语料检索与 NLA；在多数留出语言/领域上胜过 10M token 的语料检索；激活 89% 的留出 SAE 特征；识别 steering 向量概念与 NLA 相当；比语料检索更常恢复 LoRA 后门载荷；还能口述部分模型工作区内部独白。

Conclusion: MAEM 示例可通过主体模型的一次前向传播验证，因此该目标是一种有据可依（grounded）的替代方案，可用于规模化训练元模型。

Abstract: To interpret a direction in a language model's residual stream, we can look for text that strongly activates it. But when such text is found by scanning a corpus, it is limited to what that corpus contains. We develop Maximally-Activating Example Metamodels (MAEMs), language models which map residual stream directions from a subject model to text exemplars that strongly activate those directions. Our objective is to maximise the cosine similarity of a supplied residual stream direction with the activations of the text generated by the MAEM. This label-free RL objective helps to scale MAEMs on diverse sources of residual stream directions. Empirically, we find the resultant metamodel produces informative exemplars: a Qwen3.6-27B MAEM inverts held-out activations better than corpus search or a natural-language autoencoder (NLA), beats corpus search (at 10M tokens) on a majority of held-out languages/domains, and activates 89% of held-out SAE features. It also identifies concepts behind steering vectors about as well as NLAs, recovers the payloads of LoRA backdoors more often than corpus search, and can even verbalise some model workspace inner monologue. Unlike explanations produced by other metamodels (e.g. NLAs), MAEM exemplars can be verified with a forward pass on the subject model, making the MAEM objective a grounded alternative for scaling the training of metamodels.

</details>


### [146] [Same Behavior, Different Mechanism: What Finite Experiments Can Certify About Integrated Information](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08469)
*Linqian Wu,Diaoxuan He*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文研究机制级量（以IIT的整合信息φ为例）能否从有限、部分、带噪干预中可识别、连续且可认证，并在4-5单元随机网络中给出精确答案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可解释性、AI审计与机器意识评估日益依赖机制级量（系统因果转移律的泛函），但实验只能通过有限、部分且带噪的干预观测该转移律，需回答这类量何时可识别、是否连续、能否由有限数据认证。

Method: 以随机4、5单元网络（已知真值）中的IIT整合信息φ为严格测试案例，比较行为与内部记录、部分观测下的可识别性，检验φ在分区选择边界处的连续性，并用替代模型拟合干预数据，构造单侧下界认证与高维区域证明。

Result: 行为可沉默：响应延迟处输入输出律相同的循环与前馈网络分别携带0.28与0 bit，内部记录4-53次试验即可区分。部分访问可致盲：遗漏单元的精度下限0.30-1.02 bit，样本量无法降低；全异自我奇偶网络中隐藏单元仅通过保真度乘积可见，一次干预可破解该对称性。φ存在尖锐边界：随机网络中99%的边界处机制微变使其变化25-100%，约1%几乎完美拟合干预数据的替代模型误报超20%；单侧主张稳健，同一数据可认证边界两侧下界，并在64维机制区域证明φ>1.004 bit，约10^7次受控转移即以≤α的误认证概率认证。

Conclusion: 可测性是具有可计算答案的设计问题：机制级量存在行为沉默、部分访问盲区与非连续边界，但单侧下界主张可由有限干预数据可靠认证。

Abstract: Interpretability, AI auditing and assessments of machine consciousness increasingly rest on mechanism-level quantities: functionals of a system's causal transition law that describe how it computes, not what it outputs. Experiments see that law only through finite, partial and noisy interventions. We ask when such a quantity is identifiable from an experiment's access, continuous in the mechanism and certifiable from finite data, and answer exactly for a deliberately demanding case - the integrated information $\varphi$ of integrated information theory - in stochastic networks of four and five units with known ground truth. Behavior can be silent: a recurrent and a feed-forward network with identical input-output laws at their response delay carry 0.28 and 0 bits, yet internal recordings separate them within 4-53 trials. Partial access can be just as blind: in our examples, units left out of a one-step recording impose precision floors of 0.30-1.02 bits that no sample size lowers, and in all-but-self parity networks, recordings of any length see several hidden units only through the product of their fidelities - a symmetry that one intervention broke in exact checks. The measure has sharp edges: because $\varphi$ selects a partition, and the cause and effect it specifies, by one criterion and reports another, infinitesimal changes of mechanism that cross an edge change it by 25-100% at 99% of the edges we found in random networks, and about 1% of surrogate models that fit interventional data almost perfectly misreport it by more than 20%. One-sided claims survive: the same data certify a lower bound on both sides of an edge, and we prove $\varphi>1.004$ bits throughout a 64-dimensional region of mechanisms and certify it from about $10^7$ controlled transitions with false-certificate probability at most $α$. Measurability is thus a design question with computable answers.

</details>


### [147] [BUMP: Brief Unexpected Mid-Interpolation Peak](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08182)
*Melinda Flóra Kiss,Márton Muntag,Adrián Csiszárik,Dániel Varga*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文发现置换对齐神经网络线性插值中的“BUMP”现象——在插值端点附近测试精度短暂提升，并据此提出权重-预测混合集成方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前工作中插值曲线端点附近的精度增益虽隐约可见，但未被明确记录与系统分析。作者希望揭示并刻画这一现象，并加以利用。

Method: 在置换对齐（permutation-aligned）网络上进行线性插值，系统观察测试精度曲线；跨数据集、架构、训练设置及早期检查点、极端剪枝等扰动验证该现象的鲁棒性，并通过受控消融排除平凡正则化解释；进而提出将权重混合与预测空间混合结合的“weight-prediction mixed ensembling”集成方案。

Result: BUMP现象在多种数据集、架构、训练机制下稳定出现，在早期检查点和极端剪枝等显著扰动下依然存在；消融表明其无法由平凡正则化效应解释。所提集成方法在测试精度上稳定优于标准logit集成，并在校准权衡上更优。

Conclusion: BUMP是置换对齐网络插值中的稳健现象，此前未被系统记录；利用该效应构建的权重-预测混合集成体现了权重空间与预测空间混合的互补收益。

Abstract: We identify an intriguing phenomenon when linearly interpolating between permutation-aligned neural networks: test accuracy briefly improves near the interpolation endpoints before dropping toward the midpoint, which we term BUMP (Brief Unexpected Mid-interpolation Peak). We observe that BUMP appears consistently across datasets, architectures, training regimes, and remains robust under substantial perturbations such as early checkpoints and extreme pruning. Moreover, controlled ablations suggest that it cannot be attributed to trivial regularization effects. While similar near-endpoint gains are visible in prior interpolation plots, they have not been explicitly documented or systematically and thoroughly analyzed. Leveraging this effect, we propose a simple, novel ensembling scheme: weight-prediction mixed ensembling that combines weight mixing with prediction-space mixing. Across our experiments, the resulting ensembling method consistently outperforms standard logit ensembling in test accuracy and yields improved calibration trade-off, highlighting complementary benefits of weight- and prediction-space mixing.

</details>


### [148] [XStack: A Benchmark for ML Repo Migration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08472)
*Linrong Cai,Taiming Lu,Haozhe Chen,Zhuang Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出 XStack 基准，用于评估智能体将 ML 仓库从 PyTorch+NVIDIA GPU 生态迁移到 JAX+Google TPU 生态的能力，并揭示当前智能体在该任务上仍不可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ML 代码目前主要绑定在 PyTorch/NVIDIA GPU 与 JAX/Google TPU 两大生态中，跨框架、跨硬件迁移是研究者的实际需求，可解锁不同硬件资源；同时该任务涉及多框架多硬件、覆盖整仓库、且具有长程性与高难度，因而构成一个天然的评测基准。

Method: 构建 XStack 基准：每个智能体接收一个 PyTorch 仓库和一台干净的 TPU 机器，需产出一个可运行的 JAX 仓库；评判标准基于源仓库已发表的结果，并经过作者方的认证与校准。

Result: 智能体迁移仍不可靠：Claude Opus 5 通过率 75%，GPT-5.6-sol 为 45%，其余智能体均不超过 40%。

Conclusion: XStack 是将 ML 代码从单一生态束缚中解放的一步，其发布旨在使跨框架、跨硬件的可靠迁移变得可度量，从而推动该方向进展。

Abstract: ML code should run where researchers choose, not where it was first written. Today, machine learning code mainly lives in two ecosystems, PyTorch on NVIDIA GPUs and JAX on Google TPUs. Migrating repositories between them means building and testing across frameworks and hardware. This migration is (1) a practical need, since it unlocks different hardware for researchers, and (2) a natural benchmark: it involves different frameworks and hardware, covers whole repositories, and is long-horizon and challenging. We introduce XStack, a benchmark that evaluates agents on migrating ML repositories between these two ecosystems. Each agent receives a PyTorch repository and a clean TPU machine and must deliver a JAX repository. Its output passes if it meets our grading criteria, based on the source repository's published result, authenticated and calibrated by us. Migration by agents remains unreliable: Claude Opus 5 passes 75%, GPT-5.6-sol 45%, and every other agent at most 40%. XStack is a step toward freeing ML code from its ecosystem. We release XStack to make progress toward reliable migration across frameworks and hardware measurable.

</details>


### [149] [Do sEMG Foundation Models Improve Cross-Subject Generalisation?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08199)
*William Raftery,Dario Farina*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了三个sEMG基础模型在三个手势分类数据集上的表现（会话内与跨被试划分、三种微调策略），发现预训练仅在会话内场景带来增益，跨被试评估时增益基本消失；被试数少的数据集上基础模型不优于经典机器学习基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有sEMG基础模型宣称学习到跨被试、跨记录条件的通用表征，但现有评测难以判断"预训练本身"是否足以缓解sEMG中严重的跨被试过拟合问题，因此需要一个能隔离预训练贡献的受控评估。

Method: 对三个sEMG基础模型在三个手势分类数据集上进行系统评估；采用会话内（intra-session）与跨被试（inter-subject）两种数据划分；使用三种微调策略以隔离预训练的相对贡献；并在被试数量较少的数据集上引入经典机器学习基线作对照。

Result: 当训练与测试样本来自同一记录会话时，预训练提升性能；但在跨被试评估下该增益基本消失；在被试数量少的数据集上，预训练几乎无增益，经典ML基线的表现持平或超过基础模型。

Conclusion: sEMG基础模型的通用表征优势目前主要体现在会话内场景，跨被试泛化能力有限，预训练并非解决跨被试过拟合的充分手段；未来需针对跨被试评测与更严谨的基准设计进行改进。

Abstract: sEMG Foundation Models aim to learn general purpose representations over diverse subjects and recording conditions that boost performance on a variety of downstream tasks. However, existing evaluations make it difficult to discern whether pretraining alone is sufficient to combat the cross-subject overfitting which makes machine learning on sEMG so challenging. We conduct a systematic evaluation of three sEMG foundation models across three gesture classification datasets under both intra-session and inter-subject splits, using three fine-tuning strategies designed to isolate the contribution of pretraining. We find that pretraining boosts performance when train and test samples are drawn from the same recording session, but this benefit largely disappears under inter-subject evaluation. In low subject count datasets, pretraining provides little to no benefit and classical ML baselines match or exceed foundation model performance.

</details>


### [150] [Beyond Quadratic Attention: Separable Gaussian Operators for ODE Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08236)
*Carlos Boned Riera,David Romero Sanchez,Oriol Ramos Terrades*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出SSoG——用可分离的轴因子化高斯核混合替代稠密自注意力，将每层代价从O(N²)降至次二次，并在离散、权重共享、连续深度Transformer中同时提升精度、参数效率与推理吞吐。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自注意力对token数呈二次代价，限制了ViT随输入分辨率的扩展；且点积注意力的Lipschitz常数在紧输入域外无界，导致连续深度（Neural ODE）变体缺乏全局解的存在唯一性保证。

Method: SSoG是一种可分离的高斯和空间算子：以学习到的轴因子化高斯核混合取代稠密注意力，使每层代价降为N的次二次函数。固定核的SSoG具有全局Lipschitz连续性，结合Picard–Lindelöf定理可证明连续深度变体在整个积分区间上存在唯一解。实验在CIFAR-100 224×224上于匹配的离散Transformer、连续（Neural ODE）与looped-Transformer三种设定下评测，并做最高1024×1024分辨率的推理时扩展测试（匹配batch size）。

Result: 离散与连续设定下，三种模型宽度上Top-1提升2.2–4.1点，参数减少8.8–16.6%（深度与训练配方固定）；looped设定下Top-1提升5.4–8.1点。1024×1024推理时，连续深度架构吞吐最高提升154%、峰值内存降低超60%，且差距随分辨率增大而扩大；离散与looped设定下吞吐亦提升超30%。

Conclusion: SSoG是稠密注意力在离散、权重共享与连续深度Transformer变体中兼具实用性、参数效率与理论保证的替代方案。

Abstract: Self-attention carries a quadratic cost in token count, which limits how far Vision Transformers can scale with input resolution. We introduce SSoG, a separable sum-of-Gaussians spatial operator that replaces dense self-attention with a learned mixture of axis-factorized Gaussian kernels, reducing the per-layer cost from $\mathcal{O}(N^2)$ to subquadratic in $N$. Unlike dot-product self-attention, whose Lipschitz constant is unbounded outside a compact input domain, SSoG with fixed kernels is globally Lipschitz continuous, which, via the Picard--Lindelöf theorem, guarantees that the continuous-depth variant has a unique solution on the whole integration interval, a global guarantee that dense-attention counterparts lack in general. On CIFAR-100 at $224\times224$, in a matched discrete-Transformer setting and in the continuous setting, SSoG improves Top-1 accuracy by 2.2--4.1 points across three model widths while using 8.8--16.6\% fewer parameters, with depth and training recipe held fixed. In a looped-Transformer setting, SSoG improves Top-1 accuracy by 5.4--8.1 points. We further probe inference-time scaling by evaluating on inputs up to $1024\times1024$ at matched batch size: in the continuous-depth (Neural ODE) architecture, SSoG reaches up to 154\% higher throughput and over 60\% lower peak memory than dense attention, with the gap widening as resolution grows, consistent with the difference in asymptotic cost. In the discrete and looped settings, SSoG also achieves over 30\% higher throughput than dense attention at this resolution. Together, these results establish SSoG as a practical, parameter-efficient, and theoretically grounded alternative to dense attention across discrete, weight-shared, and continuous-depth Transformer variants.

</details>


### [151] [FewStepRL: Fine-tuning Few-step Diffusion Models with Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08240)
*Haitao Lin,Minsi Ren,Yang Li,Xingtong Ge,Zheyuan Hu,Peiyan Hu,Tailin Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将现有扩散模型RL目标系统改造为少步（few-step）情形，提出"变换—分解—适配"三阶段框架，在统一代码库中给出FlowGRPO、AWM、DiffusionNFT、DiffusionOPSD与ReFL的少步版本，并实验比较各类方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习已成为扩散模型的标准后训练手段，但多数RL目标每次迭代都要对当前策略做 rollout，在多步采样器下 rollout 成本占主导。少步模型可降成本，但少步模型建模的是区间平均速度，而现有RL目标是为瞬时速度+多步采样器设计的，二者不匹配。

Method: 三阶段系统改造：(1) 变换——把各目标统一表达为 $x$-空间的共同策略预测（默认在干净端，过渡/流映射目标在显式标记的层级）；(2) 分解——将各目标梯度写成"朝参考的自回归项 + 奖励增强项"之和，使方法在梯度层面归结为锚点与权重的选择；(3) 适配——由少步模型在传输末端或沿其切线计算锚点，瞬时速度从网络自身或辅助网络恢复。

Result: 在统一模块化代码库中实现了FlowGRPO、AWM、DiffusionNFT、DiffusionOPSD、ReFL的少步版本。实验显示：奖励可微且梯度可负担时，基于奖励梯度的方法最有效；否则DiffusionNFT类方法通常能在较少迭代内达到较高奖励，尽管单次迭代成本有时更高。增加推理步数在总体上无系统性收益，但部分输出在两到四倍于训练采样步数时可见改善。

Conclusion: 将RL目标适配到少步扩散模型是可行且模块化的；方法选择应依据奖励可微性与成本权衡，训练时的采样步数与推理步数不必一致，增大推理步数仅在个别样本上带来改善而非总体收益。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) has become a standard post-training method for diffusion models, but most RL objectives roll out the current policy at every iteration, and with a many-step sampler these rollouts typically dominate the cost of training. Few-step models cut this cost, yet they model an average velocity over an interval, whereas existing RL objectives are built for an instantaneous velocity with a many-step sampler. We adapt RL objectives to few-step models systematically in three phases, namely a transformation, a decomposition and an adaptation. The transformation to $x$-space expresses each objective we consider through a common policy prediction, at the clean end by default and at an explicitly marked level for transition or flow-map objectives, so that objectives stated over densities, velocities or rewards become comparable. The decomposition writes the gradient of each of these objectives as the sum of a self-regression toward a reference and a reward augmentation, so that a method reduces, at the gradient level, to a choice of anchors and weights. The adaptation computes each anchor from a few-step model at the end of its transport or along its tangent, with the instantaneous velocity recovered from the network itself or from an auxiliary network. This yields few-step versions of FlowGRPO, AWM, DiffusionNFT, DiffusionOPSD and ReFL in one modular codebase. In our experiments, reward-gradient-based methods are the most effective when the reward is differentiable and its gradient is affordable; otherwise DiffusionNFT-style methods generally reach high rewards in relatively few iterations despite a sometimes higher per-iteration cost. Extra inference steps show no systematic aggregate benefit in our experiments, although selected outputs visibly improve with two to four times as many sampling steps as in training.

</details>


### [152] [Geometric Relevance Is Not Sufficient: Evaluating Focused Post-Training Supervision in Diffusion Docking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08249)
*Michael Brown,Maryam Rahimimovassagh,Mehdi Yazdani-Jahromi,Sanjeda Sara Jennifer,Ivan Garibay*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 在固定预算下比较DiffDock的广谱继续训练与聚焦监督（笛卡尔后果导向、条件目标一致性导向）及混合策略，结果显示广谱继续训练在<5Å的Top-1成功率上最优，聚焦监督无一致优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于扩散的分子对接需在较宽的损坏分布上学习修正分数场，但不同位姿误差的结构后果差异很大。这导致训练后出现两种目标的冲突：保广谱分数场覆盖，还是把监督集中到与对接几何更相关的修正上。

Method: 以DiffDock为基础进行固定预算研究，对比三类策略：广谱继续训练；聚焦于笛卡尔后果的扭转监督；聚焦于条件目标一致性的扭转监督；以及广谱/聚焦混合监督。评估维度分离为：固定分数保真度、生成位姿质量、best-of-10采样能力、置信度选出的Top-1对接性能。

Result: 在<5Å阈值下，广谱继续训练在被训练策略中取得最高平均Top-1成功率；聚焦或混合监督在给定预算下未见一致优势。起始模型固定分数保真度更好，而广谱继续训练模型的平均Top-1成功率更高，且总体best-of-10恢复率相同。

Conclusion: 将监督聚焦于结构上更具后果性或指定更一致的修正，并不能相对广谱继续训练稳定提升返回位姿的成功率；广谱覆盖与固定分数保真度之间存在权衡。

Abstract: Diffusion-based molecular docking requires learning a corrective score field across a broad corruption distribution, yet individual pose errors can differ substantially in their structural consequences. This creates a post-training conflict between preserving broad score-field coverage and concentrating supervision on corrections that appear more relevant to docking geometry. Using DiffDock, a fixed-budget study compares broad continuation with torsional supervision focused on Cartesian consequence or conditional target consistency, and with a broad/focused mixture. Evaluation separates fixed score fidelity, generated-pose quality, best-of-10 sampling capacity, and confidence-selected Top-1 docking performance. At <5 Angstrom, broad continuation had the highest mean Top-1 success among trained policies, and focused or mixed supervision showed no consistent advantage under the tested budget. The starting model had better fixed score fidelity, whereas the evaluated broad-continuation model had higher mean Top-1 success with the same aggregate best-of-10 recovery. Focusing supervision on structurally consequential or more consistently specified corrections did not consistently improve returned-pose success over broad continuation.

</details>


### [153] [Nowcasting of Tropical Cyclone Wind Fields and Intensity from Geostationary Imagery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08255)
*Simon Donike,Pritthijit Nath,Cristina Radin,Arthur Avenas,Ruben Cartuyvels,Emiliano Diaz,William K. Jones,Anna Jungbluth*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出简单神经网络，从静止气象卫星云图直接预测二维海面风场与风暴尺度强度指标（含最大风速及其半径），通过消融实验表明由潜在空间联合预测二维场与标量指标可提升反演精度，尤其对快速增强事件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SAR可提供高分辨率台风海面风速，但时空稀疏且难以近实时获取；静止卫星时空连续但无法直接观测海面风。故需利用静止卫星影像弥补SAR的时空缺口。

Method: 构建简单神经网络，以静止卫星影像为输入，预测二维风场与风暴尺度强度标量指标；采用共享潜在空间的多任务联合预测框架，并以系列消融实验比较不同预测配置。

Result: 联合预测二维场与标量指标（共享潜在空间）优于单独预测，最大风速及其半径的反演精度提升，快速增强事件的改善尤为显著。

Conclusion: 静止卫星影像结合简单神经网络可实现近实时、时空连续的台风风场与强度估计；多任务联合学习中的表征共享是关键增益来源。

Abstract: High-resolution tropical cyclone surface wind speeds derived from synthetic aperture radar (SAR) observations can support storm structure and intensity modelling, but remain spatially and temporally sparse and unavailable near-real-time. Geostationary satellites capture cloud tops continuously in space and time, but do not directly resolve surface winds. Here, we present simple neural networks to predict 2D wind fields and storm-scale intensity metrics from geostationary imagery. Through a series of ablation studies, we find that joint prediction of 2D fields and scalar metrics from the model latent space improves the retrieval of the (radius of) maximum wind, especially for rapid intensification events.

</details>


### [154] [Dynamic Optimistic Constrained OCO with Memory via Delay Equivalence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08302)
*Mohammed Abdullah,George Iosifidis,Salah Eddine Elayoubi,Tijani Chahed*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文在全尺度上研究带记忆与时变约束的在线凸优化（COCO-M），提出一种使用不可信预测的乐观算法，通过"记忆到延迟"归约将状态相关梯度视为延迟反馈，首次给出同时适应环境非平稳性（路径长度 P_T）与预测精度的界，并在完美预测下达到 O(1) 遗憾与可观的累积约束违反保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: COCO-M 能刻画有状态在线学习与非随机控制的预算约束动态，表达力强但研究不足；已有工作依赖函数可分性等限制性假设，且无法同时适应环境非平稳性与预测质量。

Method: 提出乐观算法，引入不可信预测（untrusted predictions）以抑制通用动态遗憾与累积约束违反；核心分析工具是从"记忆"到"延迟"的归约，将状态相关梯度处理为延迟反馈；并给出该归约与"时变约束下延迟 OCO"的独立结论。

Result: 建立首个同时适应路径长度 P_T 与预测精度的界，并去除函数可分性假设；完美预测下遗憾达 O(1)；CCV 在 P_T 未知时为 O(√T)，在 P_T 已知或可在线观测时为 O(log T)；记忆消失时恢复最优 COCO 速率；最坏情形保证为次线性。

Conclusion: COCO-M 可在无限制性假设下被乐观算法有效求解，记忆—延迟归约为该问题提供了统一分析途径，并在退化情形下与已有最优结果一致。

Abstract: Online Convex Optimization with memory and time-varying constraints (COCO-M) is a recently-introduced framework that captures the dynamics of stateful online learning and non-stochastic control with budget constraints. Despite its expressive power, COCO-M remains largely understudied. In this work, we tackle this problem in its full scale, providing an optimistic algorithm that incorporates untrusted predictions to tame universal dynamic regret and cumulative constraint violation. Our analysis leverages a potent reduction from memory to delay and treats state-dependent gradients as delayed feedback. We establish the first bounds that adapt to both environment non-stationarity (path length $P_T$) and prediction accuracy, while lifting previous restrictive assumptions such as functional separability. En route to these results, we derive findings of independent interest for the memory-to-delay reduction, and for delayed OCO with time-varying constraints. The proposed framework ensures sublinear worst-case guarantees that improve with prediction accuracy, reaching $\mathcal O(1)$ regret under perfect predictions, with cumulative constraint violation (CCV) $\mathcal O(\sqrt{T})$ for unknown $P_T$ and $\mathcal O(\log T)$ for known or online observable $P_T$ , while recovering the optimal COCO rates when memory disappears.

</details>


### [155] [Interpretable many-body interaction laws for collective motion from few trajectories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08314)
*Timon S. Gutleb*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出方向性原子团簇展开（dACE），将原子团簇展开（ACE）适配到自驱动智能体的集体运动建模中，得到可系统改进、严格等变的加速度展开，并唯一分解为1体、2体、3体及更高阶相互作用项；在模拟与真实鱼群数据上均优于神经模型SocialNet。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有集体运动模型通常把社会交互简化为成对力（pairwise forces），但实证研究表明存在多体效应（many-body effects）。同时，缺乏既有系统性可改进、又严格保持等变性的多体交互建模框架。

Method: 1）将原子间势的ACE方法推广到自驱动智能体，构造dACE：对智能体加速度给出严格等变、可系统提升阶数的展开；2）展开唯一分解为1体、2体、3体及高阶项，并引入"自身项"刻画个体过去运动，通过拟合使其不吸收交互作用；3）在规则已知的二维/三维模拟集体中逐阶恢复交互律；4）与神经模型SocialNet在模拟数据及斑马鱼、金鱼（golden shiners）追踪数据上对比；5）分析不同误差度量对交互学习的影响。

Result: 在已知规则的二维和三维模拟集体中，dACE能从小数据量逐体阶（body order by body order）恢复交互律；SocialNet需更多数据，在多体或手性（chiral）规则上误差显著更大，且仅限二维。在斑马鱼与金鱼真实追踪数据上，dACE学到的规律在两个不相交数据子集间的一致性优于SocialNet。学到的规律具备可解释性：金鱼群体中，邻域变密时3体项与成对响应方向相反（拮抗）。此外发现下一步预测精度主要反映自驱动与噪声而非交互作用。

Conclusion: dACE提供了可解释、可逐阶分解且数据高效的多体交互学习框架，优于现有神经方法并适用于三维；同时指出单纯的下步预测精度不是交互学习的可靠指标，建议采用更稳健的误差度量组合来评估交互学习。

Abstract: Models of collective motion usually reduce social interactions to pairwise forces, despite empirical evidence for many-body effects. We introduce the directional atomic cluster expansion (dACE), which adapts the atomic cluster expansion (ACE) of interatomic potentials to self-propelled agents. dACE provides a systematically improvable, exactly equivariant expansion of an agent's acceleration that splits uniquely into 1-, 2-, 3-body and higher contributions, along with a self term for the agent's own past motion that is fitted so that it does not absorb the interactions. On simulated collectives in two and three dimensions with known rules, dACE recovers the interaction law body order by body order from little data, while the neural model SocialNet, the current closest competitor, needs more data, has much larger errors on many-body or chiral rules and is restricted to 2D. On real data sets of tracked zebrafish and golden shiners, the laws learned by dACE agree better between disjoint halves of the data than SocialNet's. The learned laws are interpretable, and in the shiners the 3-body term opposes the pairwise response as neighbourhoods get dense. Finally, we show that next-step prediction accuracy mostly measures self-propulsion and noise rather than the interactions, and suggest more robust combinations of error measures for interaction learning.

</details>


### [156] [Discovering Learning Rules that Shape Others](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08335)
*Hong Jun Lee,Ziyan Wang,Jun Wang,Stefanos Leonardos,Yali Du*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出DUOS，首个在一般和博弈中发现更新规则的方法，仅用自身经验即实现对手塑造，并在锦标赛中取得归一化平均分与自对弈双第一。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一般和博弈中自利学习者的纳什均衡常对所有人不利。LOLA、M-FOS等对手塑造方法虽能促成互惠合作与剥削，但部署时需访问对手的参数、策略、奖励或学习规则，实用性受限。受单智能体强化学习中元学习所得更新规则（如DiscoRL）的启发，作者希望将该框架扩展到一般和博弈，摆脱对对手内部信息的依赖。

Method: 提出Discovered Update Rule for Opponent Shaping（DUOS）：以智能体全生命周期的期望回报为目标，元训练一条更新规则；该规则仅读取智能体自身经验，用其更新内层网络，无需接触对手的策略或参数。规则与所训练的策略相分离。

Result: 所发现的更新规则在未读取对手策略或参数的情况下，表现出与对手塑造一致的行为；在锦标赛参赛方法中，归一化平均分排名第一，自对弈排名第一。

Conclusion: DUOS首次将“发现更新规则”的范式引入一般和博弈，证明仅凭自身经验即可涌现对手塑造能力，并取得锦标赛双项第一的优异表现。

Abstract: In general-sum games, self-interested learners can reach outcomes that are poor for everyone, which has motivated methods that shape the opponent's learning. Methods like Learning with Opponent-Learning Awareness (LOLA), which differentiates through the opponent's learning process, and Model-Free Opponent Shaping (M-FOS), which uses model-free optimisation methods for long-horizon opponent shaping, have enabled mutual cooperation and exploitation of learners. However, these methods require access to the opponent's parameters, policy, rewards or learning rule during deployment. In single-agent reinforcement learning, update rules discovered by meta-learning have achieved state-of-the-art results (e.g. DiscoRL). These rules are separate from the policy they train and read only the agent's own experience. We hereby extend this framework to general-sum settings by introducing Discovered Update Rule for Opponent Shaping (DUOS). To the best of our knowledge, this is the first method to discover an update rule for an agent in general-sum games. DUOS meta-trains the rule to maximise the agent's expected return over its lifetime, and updates an inner network using the agent's own experience. We show that our discovered rule exhibits behaviour consistent with opponent shaping without reading opponent policies or parameters, and ranks 1st in normalised mean score and 1st in self-play among tournament entrants.

</details>


### [157] [StrataFlux: Memory-Hierarchy-Native Low-Bit LLM Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08363)
*Jake Hyun,Ziyu Zhao,Jae-sun Seo,Mohamed S. Abdelfattah*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: StrataFlux通过协同设计精度细化表示的存储、选择与传输，从主机内存和SSD流式补充高精度数据，在保持低比特量化内存与速度优势的同时恢复精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低比特权重量化与KV缓存量化可降低大模型推理的内存与带宽开销，但会损失精度；而直接卸载高精度数据又会阻塞生成、拖慢解码速度。作者希望打破这一"精度-效率"权衡。

Method: 提出StrataFlux框架，将细化表示（refinement representation）、选择（selection）与投递（delivery）三者协同设计：选择性精度流式传输以减少传输量，将数据传输与计算重叠，并在多个解码步间复用已获取的精度数据，使生成无需等待每次传输。在独显与统一内存平台上评测2-4比特权重与2-3比特KV缓存。

Result: 2比特Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct在RTX 5090上WikiText-2困惑度从9.46降至8.40，保持量化基线95.4%的吞吐；2比特权重+KV下Qwen3-8B的AIME 2024/2025准确率从11.0%升至47.1%，GPU显存占用不变；128K上下文下，3比特权重+2比特KV的Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct在困惑度与速度上与vLLM的4比特AWQ+8比特KV相当，显存约为其一半。

Conclusion: GPU驻留精度不必成为精度上限，卸载层级延迟也不必决定解码速度；通过选择性流式补充精度可在低比特量化下同时获得高精度与高效率。

Abstract: Quantization reduces the memory and bandwidth demands of large language model (LLM) inference but can degrade accuracy; offloading higher-precision data can instead stall generation. We introduce StrataFlux, a framework that challenges this tradeoff by recovering accuracy lost to low-bit weight and key-value (KV) cache quantization while retaining quantization's memory and speed advantages. StrataFlux co-designs refinement representation, selection, and delivery to make host RAM and solid-state drives useful sources of additional precision. Its selective precision streaming reduces transfer volume, overlaps delivery with computation, and reuses fetched precision across decoding steps so generation need not wait for every transfer. We evaluate 2-4-bit weights and 2-3-bit KV-cache on dedicated GPUs and unified-memory platforms. For 2-bit Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct weights, StrataFlux reduces WikiText-2 perplexity from 9.46 to 8.40 while maintaining 95.4% of the quantized base's throughput on RTX 5090. With 2-bit weights and KV, StrataFlux raises Qwen3-8B's AIME 2024/2025 accuracy from 11.0% to 47.1% at unchanged GPU memory use. At 128K context, Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct with 3-bit weights and 2-bit KV achieves comparable perplexity and speed to vLLM's 4-bit AWQ weights and 8-bit KV on RTX 5090, using approximately half the GPU memory. These results show that GPU-resident precision need not set the accuracy ceiling, nor offloading-tier latency dictate decoding speed.

</details>


### [158] [Muir: Linear Attention with Sensitivity-Allocated Gating and Matched-Filter Readout](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08376)
*Tushaar Gangavarapu,Gil Tabak,Clemens JS Schaefer*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Muir 通过分析门控线性注意力中状态更新的敏感性（擦除远比写入不敏感、状态行范数失衡），将全部门控融合为低秩投影并以匹配滤波读出重加权状态行，同时设计数值稳定的并行更新算法，在 166M–1.34B 规模上全面超越 GDN-2 与 Transformer。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Gated DeltaNet-2 采用通道级衰减与解耦的全秩擦除/写入门，参数开销较大。作者发现状态更新对擦除的敏感度远低于写入：键激活与单位归一化使键能量集中于稀疏通道，而值写入作用于所有通道，因此擦除门可用更低秩参数化；同时慢衰减的状态行范数远大于快衰减行，会主导未加权的读出并迫使写入时进行过强擦除以抑制干扰，造成参数与性能浪费。

Method: 1) 将衰减、擦除、写入等所有门控融合为一个按敏感性分配秩的低秩投影；2) 引入匹配滤波读出门对状态行进行重加权，缓解长范数行主导读出；3) 将节省的参数重新投入到更宽的 FFN；4) 针对大 chunk 下标准 chunkwise 算法数值不稳定的问题，设计稳定的并行更新算法以高效训练。

Result: 在 166M–1.34B 参数区间，困惑度较 GDN-2 降低 1.8–2.0%、较 Transformer 降低 2.5–3.6%；常识推理准确率最高提升 1.9 分；抽取式召回优于 GDN-2。1.34B 规模下在多跳与多针召回上均优于两种基线；从 4K 训练长度外推 2,560 倍至 10M token 流而无困惑度漂移；静态读出下 1M token 的 passkey 检索接近 100%。吞吐与 GDN-2 持平，达到其 100B token 验证损失快 1.2 倍，32K 长度下训练速度比 Transformer 快 2.7 倍。

Conclusion: 对状态更新敏感性的结构性分析可有效指导门控线性注意力的参数重分配：低秩擦除门、匹配滤波读出与更宽 FFN 的组合在保持吞吐的同时显著提升质量、长上下文召回与外推能力，是优于 GDN-2 与 Transformer 的高效线性注意力架构。

Abstract: Linear attention compresses context into a fixed-size key-value state for linear-time training and constant-memory inference. Gated delta-rule models decay this state and erase its prediction for an incoming key before writing a new value; Gated DeltaNet-2 (GDN-2) generalizes this with channel-wise decay and decoupled, full-rank erase and write gates. However, we show that state updates are far less sensitive to erasure than to writes: key activation and unit normalization concentrate key energy onto a sparse subset of channels, whereas value writes scale all channels, so the erase gate can be parameterized at lower rank. Furthermore, slow-decaying state rows accumulate much larger norms than fast rows, dominating unweighted readout and forcing stronger write-time erasure to suppress interference. Guided by this analysis, we introduce Muir, which fuses all gates into a sensitivity-allocated low-rank projection, re-weights state rows with a matched-filter readout gate, and reinvests the saved parameters in a wider feed-forward network. Because standard chunkwise algorithms become numerically unstable at the large chunk sizes needed to saturate wide matrix units, we design a parallel update for stable, efficient training. Across 166M-1.34B parameters, Muir lowers perplexity by 1.8-2.0% over GDN-2 and 2.5-3.6% over Transformer, raises commonsense accuracy by up to 1.9 points, and improves extractive recall over GDN-2. At 1.34B, Muir outperforms both baselines on multi-hop and multi-needle recall, extrapolates 2,560x from its 4K training length to 10M-token streams without perplexity drift, and maintains near-100% passkey retrieval to 1M tokens with static readout. Muir achieves these gains while matching GDN-2 throughput, reaching its 100B-token validation loss 1.2x faster, and training 2.7x faster than Transformer at 32K.

</details>


### [159] [Module-Aware Optimization for Graph Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08379)
*Guy Hadad,Haggai Roitman,Moshe Eliasof,Bracha Shapira*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GraphMuon 是一个图感知优化器，将 Muon 的矩阵正交化更新推广到由图传播表示定义的度量中，缓解图结构导致的更新放大问题，并在 OGB 等基准上显著加速收敛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 不同优化器对参数更新施加不同的几何结构：Adam/AdamW 逐坐标更新，Muon 利用权重矩阵结构做正交化更新。但在 GNN 中，权重矩阵的有效几何不仅由通道混合权重决定，还受其作用的图传播表示影响。因此即便更新在矩阵意义下被良好归一化，作用于图传播特征时其层输出影响仍可能被放大，现有优化器未考虑这一缺口。

Method: 提出 GraphMuon：将 Muon 的矩阵更新适配到图传播层输入的协方差结构中。具体地，在由图传播表示定义的度量下执行正交化，并按更新对层表示产生的实际影响归一化步长，从而得到 Muon 极分解更新步的图度量类比版本。理论分析表明原始 Muon 会遭受图诱导的更新放大，而 GraphMuon 可缓解该效应。

Result: 在三个大规模 OGB 基准上，GraphMuon 相对 AdamW 实现 1.32×–2.42× 的验证损失"到目标时间"加速；在涵盖节点分类、归纳图分类与图回归的 15 组数据集–架构配置中，13 组取得更低的到目标时间，同时保持相当或更优的预测性能。

Conclusion: GraphMuon 通过将矩阵感知优化与图传播表示几何相结合，有效抑制图诱导的更新放大，提升 GNN 训练的收敛效率，并具备理论与实证支持。

Abstract: Different optimizers impose different geometries on parameter updates. Adaptive methods such as Adam and AdamW use coordinate-wise updates, while matrix-aware optimizers such as Muon utilize weight-matrix structure through orthogonalized updates. For Graph Neural Networks (GNNs), however, the effective geometry of its weight matrices is shaped not only by its learned channel-mixing weights, but also by the graph-propagated representations it acts on. Thus, even if an update is well normalized as a matrix, its effect on the layer output can be amplified when applied to graph-propagated features. To address this gap, we introduce GraphMuon, a graph-aware optimizer that adapts Muon's matrix updates to the covariance structure of graph-propagated layer inputs. GraphMuon performs orthogonalization in a metric defined by these propagated representations and normalizes step sizes according to the update's induced effect on the layer representation. We show that theoretically, vanilla Muon can suffer from graph-induced update amplification, while our GraphMuon can mitigate this effect. As such, our GraphMuon yields a graph-metric analogue of Muon's polar update step. Empirically, GraphMuon achieves $1.32\times$-$2.42\times$ validation-loss time-to-target speedups over AdamW on three large-scale OGB benchmarks. Across 15 dataset-architecture configurations spanning node classification, inductive graph classification, and graph regression, it achieves lower time-to-target in 13 cases, while maintaining competitive or superior predictive performance. Code is available at https://github.com/guy-hadad/GraphMuon.

</details>


### [160] [Synthesis of Ovarian Cancer Multi-Omics Datasets: A Biology-Informed Evaluation Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08394)
*Déborah Boyenval,Alina Malyutina,Jaana Oikkonen,Sammeli Liikkanen,Sampsa Hautaniemi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出一个生物学知情、患者不重叠的多组学合成数据评估框架，用于评判配对RNA表达与CNV谱生成质量，并在DECIDER卵巢癌纵向队列上适配三种VAE多组学整合架构进行案例研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 配对多组学癌症队列构建成本高、共享困难，合成队列可作补充资源；但生成模型架构层出不穷，缺乏判定合成数据是否保留生物学相关结构的评估标准，因此研究重点从"再提一个架构"转向"定义评估准则"。

Method: 构建生物学知情且患者不重叠（patient-disjoint）的评估框架，以真实训练集与留出集之间的一致性作为参照基准，评估各模态特异的保真度及RNA-CNV配对间的相干性，并辅以训练数据邻近性诊断；基于变分自编码器适配三种多组学整合架构以生成配对谱图，应用于DECIDER高级别浆液性卵巢癌纵向队列。

Result: 在多项保真度与相干性指标上领先的模型，同时表现出最强的训练数据邻近性，表明这些评估维度间存在一致性。

Conclusion: 该评估协议与案例研究为社区比较未来多组学生成器提供了参考基准；合成数据评估应聚焦生物学相关结构保持，而非单纯架构创新。

Abstract: Paired multi-omic cancer cohorts are costly to assemble and difficult to share, motivating synthetic cohorts as a complementary resource. As generative model development accelerates, we shift the focus from proposing yet another architecture to defining evaluation criteria that determine whether synthetic data preserve biologically relevant structure. We introduce a biology-informed and patient-disjoint framework for evaluating the generation of paired RNA expression and copy-number variation (CNV) profiles, and apply it to the longitudinal DECIDER high-grade serous ovarian cancer cohort. The framework assesses modality-specific fidelity and coherence between RNA and CNV pairs using agreement between real training and held-out data as a reference, alongside complementary training-data proximity diagnostics. We adapt three multi-omic integration architectures based on variational autoencoders to generate paired profiles. The model leading several fidelity and coherence measures also shows the strongest training-data proximity. The protocol and case study offer the community a reference for comparing future multi-omic generators.

</details>


### [161] [Conformal Prediction for Time-Dependent PDEs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08409)
*Joshua Stiller,Annika Schneider,Eyke Hüllermeier*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文指出在PDE代理模型的时间演化场景中，标准共形预测的覆盖保证会失效（系统性欠覆盖），并从理论上证明函数空间中相邻时刻分布可互奇异、离散化下总变差距离随网格分辨率指数饱和，最后提出加权共形预测以获得随时间增长视野内的有限样本覆盖保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学机器学习中不确定性量化对洪水预报、金融风险管理、热控等安全关键任务十分重要；共形预测虽提供无分布覆盖保证，但在物理与工程常见的时间相关设定下该保证会失效并导致系统性欠覆盖，因此需要刻画其失效机理并寻找补救方法。

Method: 以PDE描述的时间相关物理系统的代理模型为对象：(1) 在函数空间框架下证明任意接近时刻的边缘分布可互奇异，从而精确覆盖不可能；(2) 在离散化设定下分析分布间总变差距离随网格分辨率的增长行为；(3) 对特定设定采用有原则的加权共形预测（weighted conformal prediction）构造有限样本保证。

Result: 函数空间中相邻时刻分布可能互奇异，精确覆盖保证不可实现；离散化下总变差距离往往随网格分辨率指数饱和，从而必须对空间离散施加限制才能保证覆盖；在特定设定下，加权共形预测可为不断增长的时间视野提供有限样本覆盖保证。

Conclusion: 时间相关PDE代理模型中共形预测的失效源于分布随时间的奇异性与离散化误差，需通过空间离散约束或加权共形预测等有原则的修正，才能在实际中恢复有限样本覆盖保证。

Abstract: Uncertainty quantification is crucial in scientific machine learning, where models inform safety-critical tasks such as flood forecasting, financial risk management, and thermal control in machines. Conformal prediction provides distribution-free coverage guarantees, but in time-dependent settings common to physics and engineering, these guarantees can break down, leading to systematic undercoverage. We study this problem in the context of surrogate models for time-dependent physical systems described by partial differential equations. We prove that in a function space setting, distributions at arbitrarily close times can be mutually singular, making exact coverage guarantees impossible. We further show that in discretized settings, the total variation distance between distributions often saturates exponentially fast with the grid resolution, mandating restrictions on the spatial discretization to ensure coverage. Finally, for certain settings, we show how to use principled weighted conformal prediction to obtain finite-sample coverage guarantees over growing time horizons.

</details>


### [162] [Spectral and Subspace Dynamics of LoRA in Supervised Fine-Tuning and Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08414)
*Chenruo Liu,Zihan Wang,Yijun Dong,Qi Lei*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了LoRA在SFT与RL两阶段的动力学差异：RL使奇异谱集中且子空间旋转快，SFT在中学习率下谱扩展；机制源于组均值基线减去。据此分别定制谱处理策略，在多数设置下取得最佳性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRA在SFT与RL中常采用相近的优化器设置，但两阶段截然不同的训练动力学如何在LoRA低秩增量中体现尚不清楚，缺乏对谱与子空间演化的定量刻画，导致无法针对阶段特性设计适配的LoRA更新。

Method: 在数学推理任务上追踪LoRA更新的奇异谱与奇异子空间；逐一插值SFT与RL的组成要素以定位差异来源；构建经LoRA更新的二次多指标模型（quadratic multi-index model）进行理论分析；基于机理设计阶段专属LoRA更新：SFT中压平完整更新的谱以分散适应方向，RL中压平当前LoRA增量张成空间之外的更新部分以促进子空间旋转。

Result: RL在所有学习率下均使谱集中，SFT在中等学习率下使谱扩展；小学习率下RL的子空间旋转显著快于SFT。定位出组均值基线减去是两种对比的共同来源，理论模型揭示其通过倾斜相干拟合与梯度噪声两竞争力的平衡，导致谱集中、子空间加速旋转并迫使风险有利学习率偏低。多模型数学推理实验中，SFT与RL各自配置在多数评测设置下优于对比方法。

Conclusion: SFT与RL下LoRA更新呈现可区分的谱与子空间动力学，其根源在于组均值基线减去；据此定制的阶段专属谱压平策略能有效提升数学推理性能，表明按训练阶段动态特性设计LoRA更新具有实用价值。

Abstract: Low-rank adaptation (LoRA) is commonly used in both supervised fine-tuning (SFT) and reinforcement learning (RL), often under similar optimizer settings. Yet, how the different dynamics of the two stages manifest in the low-rank increment that LoRA optimizes remains unclear. Tracking the singular spectrum and subspaces of LoRA updates on mathematical reasoning tasks, we find that RL concentrates the spectrum at every learning rate, while SFT expands it at moderate learning rates; moreover, RL rotates the singular subspaces much faster than SFT at small learning rates. Interpolating between SFT and RL one ingredient at a time, we pinpoint group-mean baseline subtraction as the source of both contrasts. A quadratic multi-index model updated via LoRA unveils the underlying mechanism: group-mean baseline subtraction tilts the balance between two competing forces, coherent fitting and gradient noise, toward spectral concentration and faster subspace rotation, while forcing the risk-favorable learning rate to be low. Guided by these dynamics, we tailor LoRA updates to each stage: in SFT, we flatten the spectrum of the full update to spread adaptation across singular directions, whereas in RL, we flatten the part of the update outside the current LoRA increment span to promote subspace rotation. Experiments on mathematical reasoning tasks across multiple models show that the respective configurations for SFT and RL yield the best performance among the compared methods in most evaluated settings.

</details>


### [163] [On the limitations of molecular representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08420)
*Acer Blake,Fergus Imrie,Charlotte M. Deane*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了分子表示（指纹、学习嵌入、物理化学描述符）的质量，发现现有先进分子嵌入模型在捕捉图结构、3D形状和ADMET端点相似性上均不及指纹或描述符，同时经典指纹在超大化合物库中存在大量非唯一表示（Enamine REAL 10.8B分子中225M~4B）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 分子表示（指纹、学习嵌入）是化学信息学和小分子机器学习中聚类、数据集划分、虚拟筛选等任务的核心输入，但其评估通常仅限于狭窄基准任务上的下游模型性能，缺乏对表示本身质量的系统考察。

Method: 对多种分子表示的质量开展系统性研究，评估其捕捉图结构、3D形状及ADMET端点相似性的能力，并分析指纹在Enamine REAL数据库中非唯一表示的比例。

Result: 当前最先进的分子嵌入模型在捕捉图结构、3D形状、ADMET端点相似性方面均不如指纹或物理化学描述符；经典指纹亦存在实质性局限——在10.8B的Enamine REAL库中，依表示不同有225M至约4B分子具有非唯一表示，对虚拟筛选产生影响。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了当前分子表示的局限性，并指出未来需要发展更稳健的小分子编码方向。

Abstract: Molecular representations such as fingerprints and learned embeddings play a central role in cheminformatics and small-molecule machine learning. They serve as the primary input for a diverse array of tasks, including clustering, dataset splitting, and virtual screening, yet their evaluation is often limited to downstream model performance on narrow benchmark tasks. We carried out a systematic investigation of the quality of molecular representations, finding that none of the state-of-the-art molecular embedding models capture graph structure, 3D shape, or ADMET endpoint similarity better than fingerprints or physicochemical descriptors. However, classical fingerprints also revealed their own material limitations. For example, we show that depending on the representation, between 225M and $\sim$4B molecules have non-unique representations in the 10.8B Enamine REAL database, with implications for virtual screening. Our findings provide an insight into the current limitations of molecular representations, outlining future research directions for the development of more robust encodings of small molecules.

</details>


### [164] [A Hidden Markov Preference Model for Online Prediction of Adversary Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08445)
*Angel Daruna,Michael Cogswell,Tixiao Shan,Yi Yao,Han-Pang Chiu,Ajay Divakaran*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出以攻击者行为机制为隐状态的隐马尔可夫模型，将机制条件动作律定义在可行性掩码的可执行动作树上，实现下一步动作分布预测与机制信念更新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 防御方需在攻击者行动前给出其下一步动作的概率分布，但存在两个复杂因素：攻击者行为机制会持续且可在单局内切换；每个动作由操作与依赖状态的参数组成，可行取值随时间变化。

Method: 构建隐马尔可夫模型，隐状态为当前活跃机制，机制条件动作律定义在可行性掩码的可执行动作树上；同一似然既构成下一步动作分布，又在观测到动作后更新机制信念，部分观测前缀贡献已解析的局部因子；估计分解为凸逻辑回归拟合，机制无标签时由Baum-Welch复用。

Result: 给出机制的到达加权逐节点KL信息表达、切换机制路径的序列级对数损失界以及均匀回退保证；在CAGE 2网络防御基准上，滤波隐状态每步价值0.8 nats，控制器切换后损失三步内开始下降，而循环基线需十步。

Conclusion: 所提可行性掩码隐马尔可夫建模框架可有效刻画机制切换与状态相关动作空间，兼具理论保证与预测/自适应优势。

Abstract: A defender anticipating an attacker needs a probability distribution over the attacker's next action from information available before that action occurs. Two structures complicate this: the attacker's behavioral regime persists and can switch within an episode, and each action combines an operation with state-dependent arguments whose feasible values change over time. We capture both with a hidden Markov model whose latent state is the active regime and whose regime-conditioned action law is defined on a feasibility-masked tree of executable actions. The same likelihood forms the next-action distribution and updates the regime belief once the action is seen, and a partially observed prefix contributes its resolved local factors. Estimation separates into convex logistic fits that Baum-Welch reuses when regimes are unlabeled. We derive a reach-weighted nodewise KL expression for regime information, a sequence-wise log-loss bound for switching regime paths, and a uniform fallback guarantee. On the CAGE 2 cyber defense benchmark the filtered latent state is worth 0.8 nats per step, and after a controller switch the loss begins to fall in three steps against ten for a recurrent baseline.

</details>


### [165] [Unpacking the Compute Efficiency of Strong-to-Weak OPD against GRPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08453)
*Zeyu Leo Liu,Yang Li,Bo Pang,Emre Can Acikgoz,Xuan-Phi Nguyen,Anurag Koul,Srijan Bansal,Erik Nijkamp,Shafiq Joty,Semih Yavuz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 该文系统比较on-policy蒸馏(OPD)与GRPO的"每训练FLOP精度"效率，发现外部教师下OPD普遍不如GRPO；但若教师是学生自身GRPO检查点，OPD能用少50倍FLOPs复现其精度；据此提出多教师OPD聚合各领域专家，蒸馏成本仅占专家训练FLOPs的5.2%–8.0%，2域混合下总FLOPs减少42.0%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OPD提供冻结教师逐token的稠密监督，直觉上应比RL的稀疏奖励更省算力，但"稠密信号是否真的降低达标所需训练FLOPs"缺乏系统性、以算力为横轴的对照证据。作者希望在任务难度、师生规模、领域与优化选择等维度上公平比较OPD与GRPO，并弄清教师选择如何决定OPD的效率。

Method: 在匹配设置下对比vanilla reverse-KL OPD与GRPO，以held-out精度对训练FLOPs作图衡量效率；扫描任务难度、学生/教师规模、领域及优化配置；并变换教师来源（外部post-trained教师、经GRPO继续训练的教师、学生自身的GRPO检查点），进一步构造多教师OPD把各领域GRPO专家整合为单一学生。

Result: 外部post-trained教师下，OPD在匹配设置中精度/FLOP普遍劣于GRPO，即使教师再用GRPO训练亦然，且常出现回复长度膨胀。当教师为学生自身GRPO检查点时，OPD在数学上以约50×更少蒸馏FLOPs复现该检查点精度；在更大模型、代码领域及第二个模型族中同样观察到该高效率。单领域情形下该检查点本身已是目标模型，蒸馏反而增加总算力。多教师OPD聚合逐域专家，学生匹配各专家精度，蒸馏阶段仅耗专家训练FLOPs的5.2%(2域)/8.0%(4域)；计入专家训练后，2域混合以42.0%更少总FLOPs达到联合GRPO的性能。

Conclusion: OPD的算力效率高度依赖教师来源：外部教师不可替代RL；但自蒸馏GRPO检查点极具效率，且多教师OPD可作为将领域专家合并为单模型的低成本方案。效率收益是相对"复用已有专家"而言的，须将专家训练FLOPs计入总成本评估，且跨领域需多个专家、无单一检查点可覆盖全部领域。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) trains a student on its own rollouts under a frozen teacher's per-token supervision. Does this dense signal reduce the training FLOPs needed to reach a target accuracy compared with reinforcement learning? We systematically compare vanilla reverse-KL OPD with Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO) across task difficulty, student and teacher size, domain, and optimization choices, measuring held-out accuracy against training FLOPs. With an external post-trained teacher, OPD generally loses to GRPO in accuracy per training FLOP at matched settings, even when the teacher is further trained with GRPO, and often suffers response-length inflation. Yet a different teacher changes the picture: when the teacher is the student's own GRPO checkpoint, OPD recovers that checkpoint's accuracy on math with $50\times$ fewer distillation FLOPs than GRPO needs to reach the same accuracy. Following the same teacher setting, we consistently observe this high efficiency with a larger student, on code, and in a second model family. On a single domain, however, the GRPO checkpoint is already the desired model, so distilling it adds to total compute. Across domains, separate GRPO checkpoints hold different capabilities, and no single checkpoint serves every domain. Multi-teacher OPD (DeepSeek-AI, 2026) consolidates per-domain GRPO experts into a single student that matches each expert's accuracy, with the distillation stage costing only 5.2% (on 2 domains) and 8.0% (on 4 domains) of the specialist training FLOPs. Counting specialist training and distillation together, this pipeline reaches joint GRPO's performance on the 2-domain mix with 42.0% fewer total FLOPs.

</details>


### [166] [Adversarial Training with Large Step Sizes: Implicit Bias, Optimization, and Sharpness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08457)
*Yi Feng,Andrea Paudice*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究对抗训练(AT)中确定性梯度下降(GD)在大常数步长下的动力学，揭示其与标准训练(ST)的三个关键差异：隐式偏差差距在任意大常数步长下持续存在、AT优化误差不存在一致的收敛速率、以及锐度呈现不同于边缘稳定(EoS)行为的两阶段动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 既有对AT中GD动力学的研究主要集中于小步长或消失步长情形（此时GD近似连续时间流），但这类步长训练效率低、实践中很少使用。本文因此关注实践更常用的大常数步长下AT的GD动力学。

Method: 首先在对抗线性逻辑回归中研究GD，聚焦两方面：模型参数收敛到哪里（隐式偏差）以及损失下降多快（优化）；随后转向神经网络训练，研究AT过程中损失锐度(sharpness)的演化。

Result: 发现与ST的三个关键差异：(i) AT与ST之间此前仅在小步长下建立的隐式偏差差距，在任意大常数步长下依然存在；(ii) 与ST不同，AT中优化误差不存在一致的收敛速率；(iii) 锐度可呈现两阶段动力学，区别于ST中观察到的边缘稳定(EoS)行为。

Conclusion: 本文推进了对AT中大常数步长下GD行为的理解，表明大步长AT的动力学与ST在小步长下的直觉存在本质差异，为理解实用化对抗训练的优化与泛化特性提供了理论基础。

Abstract: Adversarial Training (AT) is an important method for improving model robustness. We study the dynamics of deterministic Gradient Descent (GD) in AT. Most existing works in this direction focus on the small or vanishing step sizes regime, where GD resembles a continuous-time flow. However, such step sizes can make training inefficient and are rarely used in practice. In this paper, we study the AT dynamics of GD with large constant step sizes. We first study GD in adversarial linear logistic regression, focusing on two aspects: where the model parameters converge (implicit bias) and how quickly the losses decrease (optimization). We then turn to neural networks training and study how the sharpness of losses evolve during AT. Our results advance the understanding of the behaviors of GD with large step sizes in AT and highlight three key differences from its behavior in Standard Training (ST): (i) the implicit bias gap between AT and ST, previously established only for small step sizes, persists for arbitrarily large constant step sizes, (ii) different from ST, there is no uniform rate of convergence for the optimization error in AT, and (iii) sharpness can exhibit two-stage dynamics that differ from the Edge-of-Stability behavior observed in ST.

</details>


### [167] [User Embeddings are Superpositions of Interpretable Behavioral Modes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.08487)
*Jonathan Kozaczuk,Zheng Dong,Noyan Evirgen,Keivan Majidi,Eddie K. H. Ng,Chris Porter,Qi Zhao,Jia Geng,Changmao Li,Brandon R. Feng*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 用户嵌入可视为可解释行为模式的稀疏叠加，可用稀疏自编码器等稀疏字典学习方法恢复并自动命名，从而实现零样本受众检索与刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户嵌入虽能紧凑编码丰富行为信息，但本质稠密且不透明，可解释性受限，因此需要一种事后、模型无关的方法来解析其内部结构。

Method: 提出将用户嵌入理解为可解释行为模式的稀疏叠加（该结构由线性嵌入的行为生成模型直接预测，并经叠加假设近似推广到深度表示）；采用稀疏字典学习（如稀疏自编码器 SAE）恢复叠加模式，并对恢复的模式进行自动自然语言命名与查询；在合成数据（含 transformer 编码嵌入）上与经典稀疏基线对比验证，并应用于两个真实数据集中的经典与 transformer 用户嵌入。

Result: 合成数据上 SAE 的模式恢复优于经典稀疏基线，且对 transformer 编码嵌入同样有效；真实数据集上成功恢复模式；自然语言标签支持对从未用文本训练的嵌入进行零样本受众检索与刻画。

Conclusion: 该框架为事后、模型无关，无需修改上游嵌入系统，即可提升用户嵌入的可解释性并拓展其应用。

Abstract: User embeddings are central components of modern recommendation, personalization, and behavioral modeling systems. Though these embeddings can compactly encode rich behavioral information, they are inherently dense and opaque, limiting interpretability. To address this gap, we argue that user embeddings can be understood as sparse superpositions of interpretable behavioral modes. This structure is directly predicted by generative models of user behavior for linear embeddings and extends approximately to deep-learning representations by the superposition hypothesis. Superimposed modes can naturally be recovered by sparse dictionary learning methods, such as sparse autoencoders (SAEs), and recovered modes can be automatically named and queried in natural language. We validate mode recovery against known ground truth on synthetic data, where SAEs recover modes better than classical sparse baselines, including on transformer-encoded embeddings. We then apply the method to classical and transformer-based user embeddings from two real-world datasets. We also show how the corresponding natural-language labels enable zero-shot audience retrieval and characterization over embeddings never trained on text. The framework is post-hoc and model-agnostic, requiring no modification to the upstream embedding system.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [168] [Formalizing inverse problems in the age of AI: possibilities, pitfalls, and an invitation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06962)
*Christian Clason,Tapio Helin,Medet Nursultanov,Lauri Oksanen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文介绍 AutoIP——一个由 AI 智能体自动形式化的 Lean 库，覆盖经典线性正则化理论及其依赖的泛函分析，并以"审阅命题而非审阅证明"为组织原则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Lean 等证明助手可将数学证明检验到公理级别，AI 智能体已使形式化证明的撰写成本大幅降低；但机器检验只保证所证形式命题为真，不保证命题表达了原本意图，因此人的剩余任务是对照自然语言确认每条形式命题的含义。

Method: 构建 AutoIP 库：由 AI 智能体自动形式化线性正则化理论与所需泛函分析；以形式命题为中心组织库结构，使审阅者只需比对形式陈述与自然语言原文而无需阅读证明；并对命题施加简单的自动化测试（对命题的可判定性质做自动检验）以暴露表述问题。

Result: 经验表明风险主要位于命题陈述而非证明；简单的命题级自动化测试可发现即便是成熟文献中的问题。作者以一本教科书中的定理为例加以说明：其实际假设比原文所声称的更为严格。

Conclusion: 形式化的工作重心应从"证明是否可靠"转向"陈述是否忠实"；作者邀请反问题社区扩展并在此基础上继续构建 AutoIP 库。

Abstract: Proof assistants such as Lean check mathematical proofs down to the axioms, and AI agents have recently made writing such proofs cheap. We describe AutoIP, a Lean library in which classical linear regularization theory, together with the functional analysis it rests on, has been autoformalized by AI agents. A machine-checked proof certifies only the formal statement it proves, so the remaining human task is to confirm that each formal statement says what was intended. We organize the library around this task: a reviewer compares the formal statements with their natural-language counterparts and never needs to read a proof. Our experience is that the risks lie in the statements rather than in the proofs, and that simple automated tests on the statements can expose problems even in the established literature. We illustrate this with a textbook theorem whose hypothesis is more restrictive than asserted. We invite the inverse problems community to extend and build on the library.

</details>


### [169] [KDASO: Knowledge-Data Dual-Driven Automated Skill Optimization for Liability Adjudication Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06972)
*Zi-Jian Cheng,Weiming Wu,Jie Meng,Zhen Peng,Shan Huang,Qun Li,Guobing Wu,Lan-Zhe Guo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出知识-数据双驱动的自动化技能优化方法 KDASO，通过邻域条件诱导、执行对齐精炼与上下文预算路由，持续演化技能库，在网约车判责、法律责任认定与逻辑推理判断任务上显著优于通用与领域基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 判责类任务需依据领域技能将事件描述与责任归属对应；现有基于可执行技能的可解释 LLM 推理存在三处缺陷：技能库随真实场景演化而陈旧、技能描述存在"语义-执行鸿沟"（LLM 偏离专家逻辑）、技能规模增长超出上下文预算导致关键技能遗漏。

Method: KDASO 三组件：(1) 基于近期判责数据的邻域条件归纳（neighborhood-conditioned induction）实现技能库增量演化；(2) 基于 LLM 推理轨迹分析的执行对齐精炼，弥合语义-执行鸿沟；(3) 上下文预算约束下的技能路由，实现可扩展的技能选择。

Result: 在网约车判责、法律责任认定、逻辑推理判断三类任务上显著超越通用与领域专用基线；技能迁移至概念相似场景提升 8.96%；持续优化下保持稳定增益。

Conclusion: 知识-数据双驱动闭环优化可生成高可迁移、稳定的领域技能库，兼顾可解释推理与规模化部署，为动态演化场景下的判责类任务提供可行范式。

Abstract: Liability adjudication tasks (e.g., legal charge determination) require analyzing event descriptions against domain-specific skills to determine whether a party should bear liability. While Large Language Models (LLMs) have shown promise in this area by leveraging executable skills for interpretable reasoning, existing approaches face three critical limitations: skill libraries become stale as real-world scenarios dynamically evolve, skill descriptions often suffer from a semantic execution gap where LLMs deviate from intended expert logic, and growing skill collections exceed context budgets causing critical skill omission. To address these challenges, we propose Knowledge-Data Dual-Driven Automated Skill Optimization (KDASO), which continuously evolves a skill library through neighborhood-conditioned induction from recent adjudication data, execution-aligned refinement based on LLM trajectory analysis, and context-budgeted routing for scalable skill selection. Experiments across ride-hailing adjudication, legal liability determination, and logical reasoning judgment demonstrate that KDASO significantly outperforms current general-purpose and domain-specific baselines. KDASO further achieves 8.96% improvement when transferring skills to concept-similar scenarios and maintains consistent performance gains through continuous optimization, underscoring the high transferability and stability of generated skills.

</details>


### [170] [BlockLock: Reproducible LLM Inference with Fixed Batch Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06977)
*Xiuyuan Qi,Pedro Reviriego,Javier Conde,Shanshan Liu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: BlockLock 通过仅固定受保护请求所见的数值几何，在保留共享批量 prefill 的同时实现逐位可复现的 LLM 推理，延迟降低 53.5–55.0%、吞吐提升 115.2–122.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可复现 LLM 推理要求同一请求的输出不受同批其他请求影响。现有方案代价高：batch-invariant kernels 约束所有算子；固定形状 decode 隔离 prefill 的方案放弃共享 prefill 计算并对 decode 填充。

Method: 提出 BlockLock，保留共享批量 prefill，仅钉住受保护请求的数值路径，固定该请求所见几何。两种模式：BlockLock-Rows 使用固定宽度行，保留所有请求的批量 decode；BlockLock-Pack 使用末端锚定打包加隔离哑段，随后提取受保护请求的 KV 状态做 target-only decode。在给定的每部署契约下，伴随请求变化时两种模式均保持目标输出逐位一致，且无需自定义 kernel。

Result: 在五个 dense 模型（Qwen3-8B/14B、Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct、Gemma-3-4B/12B-IT）、三种单 GPU 部署（A6000、A100、H100）上于 tinyBenchmarks 与 BFCL 评测，并含双 A6000 张量并行初步实验；两种模式均保持目标序列与 Top20 logit 位一致。相同交付工作量下，BlockLock-Rows 相比 vLLM Batch-Invariant 延迟降低 53.5–55.0%，吞吐提升 115.2–122.2%。

Conclusion: 仅约束受保护请求而非全部算子，即可在保持共享 prefill 与批量 decode 效率的同时获得逐位可复现性，显著优于 batch-invariant kernels 且无需定制实现。

Abstract: Reproducible LLM inference requires that a request produces the same output regardless of the other requests in the batch. Existing solutions pay for this in different ways: batch-invariant kernels constrain every operator, and isolating prefill with fixed-shape decode ({our CoRun-style baseline}) gives up shared prefill compute and pads decode. We present BlockLock, which keeps shared batched prefill and pins only the numerical path of the protected request: it fixes the geometry that the request sees, with {two modes: BlockLock-Rows uses fixed-width rows and retains batched decode of all requests; BlockLock-Pack uses terminal-anchored packing with an isolated dummy segment, then extracts the protected request's KV state for target-only decode. Under a stated per-deployment contract, both modes preserve the target's output bitwise when its companions change.} Across five {dense} models (Qwen3-8B/14B, Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, and Gemma-3-4B/12B-IT) and three single-GPU deployments (NVIDIA A6000, A100, and H100), we evaluate our approach on both tinyBenchmarks and BFCL{; we also include initial tensor-parallel experiments on two NVIDIA A6000 GPUs. Both modes preserve target sequences and Top20-logit bits in the reported tests.} At equal delivered work, {BlockLock-Rows} reduces latency by 53.5--55.0\% and improves throughput by 115.2--122.2\% relative to vLLM Batch-Invariant; compared with batch-invariant kernels, it needs no custom kernel implementations and constrains only the protected requests.

</details>


### [171] [Resource-Efficient Reinforcement Learning Post-Training through Elastic Expert Parallelism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06984)
*Jia Wei,Ziyi Qiu,Yi Xu,Xuehai Qian*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: FlexFloor：面向MoE大模型RL后训练中同步响应生成阶段的资源释放系统，通过联合调整响应调度与活跃worker组，在保证进行中响应所需专家计算与通信正确性的前提下提前释放已完成本地生成的worker。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RL后训练能提升大模型能力，但生成训练响应的开销是主要计算瓶颈。在MoE模型中，先完成响应的worker因其所持专家仍被处理长响应的worker需要而无法退出，基线中约三分之一的占用worker时间发生在本地响应生成结束之后，造成显著资源浪费。

Method: 提出FlexFloor系统：利用历史响应长度与内存约束指导批处理构造与prompt放置；联合自适应响应调度与活跃worker组；在生成过程中渐进式释放worker，同时维持专家可用性并保证通信正确性。

Result: 相比verl基线，单位占用计算时间生成的响应token数提升约65.40%，响应生成吞吐提升52.74%，rollout reward基本无明显波动。

Conclusion: FlexFloor通过提升RL后训练效率，支撑大模型与AI智能体的更快研发与迭代改进。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning post-training improves the capabilities of large language models, but generating training responses is a major computational bottleneck. In mixture-of-experts models, workers that finish their assigned responses may remain occupied because their experts are still needed by workers processing longer responses. Consequently, approximately one-third of occupied worker time in the baseline occurs after local response generation finishes. Here we present FlexFloor, a system that enables earlier resource release during synchronous response generation while preserving the expert computation required by ongoing responses. FlexFloor jointly adapts response scheduling and the active group of workers. It uses historical response lengths and memory constraints to guide batching and prompt placement, then progressively releases workers while maintaining expert availability and correct communication. Compared with the verl baseline, FlexFloor generates approximately 65.40% more response tokens per unit of occupied computing time and increases response generation throughput by 52.74%, with little observed variation in rollout reward. By making reinforcement learning post-training more efficient, FlexFloor supports faster development and iterative improvement of large language models and AI agents.

</details>


### [172] [The Two-Ledger Model: Transaction-Grain Cost Accounting for Multi-Agent AI Systems Across Clouds, Models, and Systems of Record](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.06989)
*Vivek Acharya*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文指出企业AI成本结算粒度与业务交易粒度之间存在错配，提出"双账本模型"：将FOCUS合规的结算账本与基于OpenTelemetry GenAI遥测的调用粒度归因账本关联，使AI成本可按订单/理赔/案件等业务交易口径报告。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业通过跨云、LLM、编排框架和记录系统的多智能体AI系统运行业务，但财务只能获得按SKU和计费周期聚合的结算颗粒度AI成本；服务商看不到企业交易边界，因此"这笔订单/理赔/案件花了多少AI成本"无法从账单数据回答。FOCUS虽统一了多厂商计费schema并新增token与请求方归因，但仍是结算粒度。

Method: 形式化定义结算粒度与交易粒度的错配，推导七项需求，提出双账本模型：FOCUS合规结算账本 + OpenTelemetry GenAI遥测的调用粒度归因账本；采用分层关联脊（trace context、A2A/MCP元数据、业务键关联、概率匹配）为每条记录赋业务交易并记录方法与置信度；版本化费率卡近实时计价；周期性对账将两账本与发票对齐，残差识别影子AI；以带已知真值的仿真验证。

Result: 仿真显示：trace传播降至零时关联脊仍维持99%以上成本覆盖率；对账残差在0.24个百分点内回收植入的未计量支出，同时标记服务商计费错误；中途门控将p99交易成本最高降低61%，误暂停率2–4%。

Conclusion: 双账本模型可实现按订单、理赔或业务结果的成本报告与智能体成本指标；作者建议将归因账本作为调用粒度的FOCUS profile提交标准化。

Abstract: Enterprises run business transactions through multi-agent AI systems spanning clouds, large language models (LLMs), orchestration harnesses, and systems of record. Finance receives AI cost only at settlement grain, aggregated by SKU and charge period. The business question (what did this order, claim, or case cost in AI?) cannot be answered from billing data, because providers never see enterprise transaction boundaries. The FinOps Open Cost and Usage Specification (FOCUS) gives billing data one schema across providers but remains settlement-grain even as it adds AI token and requester attribution. We formalize this grain mismatch, derive seven requirements, and propose the Two-Ledger Model: a settlement ledger of FOCUS-conformant billing data joined to an attribution ledger of invocation-grain usage records built on OpenTelemetry GenAI telemetry. A layered correlation spine (trace context, A2A/MCP metadata, business-key correlation, probabilistic matching) assigns each record to its business transaction with recorded method and confidence; a versioned rate card prices usage in near real time; period reconciliation ties both ledgers to the invoice, and the unexplained residual identifies shadow AI. Costs are reported per order, claim, or outcome with agentic cost metrics.
